सं. 58--937/2025—ared की राष्ट्रपति, उत्तर प्रदेश गृह रक्षक एवं नागरिक सुरक्षा सेवा के निम्नलिखित कार्मिक को वीरता पदक 
(जीएम) सहर्ष प्रदान करती हैं:-- 


क्र.सं. पदक पाने वाले का नाम कार्रवाई के समय पद प्रदान किया गया पदक 


t. श्री अमरनाथ मिश्रा चीफ वार्डन वीरता पदक 


उन सेवाओं का विवरण, जिनके लिए अलंकरण प्रदान किया गया 


श्री अमरनाथ मिश्रा, सिविल डिफेंस, लखनऊ के चीफ वार्डन हैं। उन्होंने जिला स्तर और राष्ट्रीय स्तर पर आपदाओं से संबंधित कई 
प्रशिक्षण कार्यक्रमों में भाग लिया और उनका आयोजन किया है। दिनांक 08.09.2024 को लखनऊ के याहियागंज बाजार में शॉर्ट सर्किट के कारण 
एक इमारत की चौथी मंजिल पर स्थित एक घर में आग लगने से एक भयावह स्थिति पैदा हो गई। मकान के मालिक सुधाकर बाजपेयी, उनके 
भाई दिवाकर बाजपेयी और परिवार के अन्य सदस्य आग को देखकर घबराने लगे थे। याहियागंज बाजार भीड़भाड़ वाला क्षेत्र था और इस क्षेत्र में 
बचाव के लिए मौजूद सड़कें बहुत संकरी थीं। श्री अमरनाथ मिश्रा तुरंत घटनास्थल पर पहुंचे और उन्होंने इलाके को खाली कराना शुरू किया। 
इसके बाद, कुछ स्थानीय स्वयंसेवकों की मदद से उन्होंने प्रभावित क्षेत्र की घेराबंदी की। फिर, वे तुरंत उस इमारत की चौथी मंजिल की ओर दौड़े, 
जहां आग लगी थी। उन्होंने मुख्य लाइन से इमारत की बिजली की आपूर्ति काटी और मकान मालिक श्री सुधाकर के परिवार के सभी सदस्यों को 
घटनास्थल से सकुशल निकालकर इमारत के बाहर एक सुरक्षित स्थान पर पहुंचाया। जिस कमरे से आग फैली थी वहां जहरीला धुआं भरा हुआ 
था। उन्होंने अपनी नाक पर एक रूमाल रखकर उस घर में प्रवेश किया। उन्होंने देखा कि रसोई में आग लगी है, जहां दो एलपीजी गैस सिलेंडर 
रखे हुए थे। अगर सिलेंडरों में आग लग जाती, तो स्थिति और भी भयावह हो सकती थी। उन्होंने तुरंत दोनों सिलेंडरों को घर से बाहर निकालकर 
सुरक्षित स्थान पर रखा और आग पर बालटी से पानी डालना शुरू किया। कुछ ही देर में, घर में लगी आग बुझा दी गई। श्री अमरनाथ मिश्रा की 
सजगता और त्वरित निर्णय लेने की क्षमता के कारण, आग पर जल्द ही काबू पा लिया गया और कोई हताहत नहीं हुआ। 


इस ऑपरेशन में, उत्तर प्रदेश गृह रक्षक एवं नागरिक सुरक्षा सेवा के श्री अमरनाथ मिश्रा, चीफ वार्डन ने अदम्य वीरता, साहस और 
उच्चकोटि की कर्तव्यपरायणता का परिचय दिया। 


वीरता पदक (जीएम), राष्ट्रपति सचिवालय की अधिसूचना संख्या 85-प्रेज/2023 दिनांक 44 अक्टूबर, 2023 और गृह मंत्रालय के पत्र 
संख्या 024/06/2023-tivau दिनांक 46 अक्टूबर, 2023 के तहत प्रदान किया जाता Sl फलस्वरूप, इस पुरस्कार में शामिल स्वीकार्य 
वित्तीय भत्ता दिनांक 08.09.2024 से दिया जाएगा। 


(फाइल AeAT-7020/96/2024-FiraT)

No. 85—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 


personnel of Assam Police:— 


Sl.No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
l. Gurav Abhijit Dilip, IPS Superintendent of Police GM 
2: Hemanta Kumar Boro Sub Divisional Police Officer GM 
Akhil Ranjan Das Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 8th February 2023, based on a reliable input about one group of ULFA (I) extremist taking shelter at Malu Gaon 
Hills, Ledo under Margherita Police station with an intention to cause sabotage activities; an operation was launched led by 
IGP, NER along with SP Tinsukia Gurav Abhijit Dilip, IPS, SDPO Margherita Hemanta Kumar Boro, Constable Akhil Ranjan 
Das along with Police team. The police team proceeded to the area at around 9.00 PM and reached to the hills at midnight. 
After detailing cover party in strategic locations, the police team while climbing up the hills to recce the area, an extremist 
group camping on the upper elevation, started indiscriminate fire upon them, however the team managed to duck in the hilly 
terrain without hurt. Thereafter, the police team coordinated accompanying team and approached closer to the extremists in 
spite of heavy fire. When the exact position of the extremists was ascertained, the police team retaliated the fire with aggressive 
support of accompanying team, thereby a fierce exchange of fire ensued and which was lasted for |5-20 minutes. Though the 
exchange of fire was stopped yet the police team remained in lying position for sometimes. But when sound of groaning of a 
person was heard, the police team crawled up to the place and saw an injured extremist, armed with AK rifle in lying position. 
The injured extremist was lifted and rushed to Margherita FRU, Hospital for treatment. In the hospital, doctor on duty declared 
him brought dead. The deceased was later identified as Udoy Axom @ Uddhab Lahon, an Operation Commander of United 
Liberation Front of Assam (Independent). Rest of the extremists managed to flee. A thorough search of the area was conducted 
in the first light where arms, ammunitions and materials were recovered. 


It is to be mentioned that during the operation it was completely dark, rainy and the terrain was difficult to navigate, 
despite of all the odds, the operation resulted in success cheerily for meticulous planning, tactical approach, courage and 
leadership displayed by SP Gurav Abhiyit Dilip, IPS, SDPO Hemanta Kumar Boro and Constable Akhil Ranjan Das. 


In this operation, S/Shri Gurav Abhiit Dilip, IPS, Superintendent of Police, Hemanta Kumar Boro, Sub Divisional 
Police Officer and Akhil Ranjan Das, Constable of Assam Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to 
duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 09/02/2023. 


(File No. 020/69/2024-PMA) 


S.M. SAMI 
Under Secretary

personnel of Bihar Police:— 


Sl.No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
i Babu Ram, IPS Superintendent of Police GM 
Ze Saket Saurabh Sub-Inspector GM 
3. Ram Raj Singh Sub-Inspector GM 
4. Tarbabu Yadav Sub-Inspector GM 


PART [-.86०. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 695 


5: Sanjay Kumar Choudhary Constable GM 
| 56. | Surendra Paswan Constable GM 
hi Vikas Kumar Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 6-07-206, Shri Babu Ram, IPS, SP Aurangabad got indulgence about camping of an armed group of Maoists 
led by their Commander BJSAC Member Sandeep jee and others in nearby areas of Langurahi yillage situated in 
Chhakarbandha forest under Madanpur PS, with intention to execute some explosive plan. 


Chbakrabandha forest the most infamous den of Maoists, is sprawled in Aurangabad and Gaya districts of Bihar. All 
kachcha tracks are prone to IEDs placed on tactical locations along the heights of both sides of these tracks. These heights 
provide safe cover to Maoists to fire on security forces, whereas security forces have no choice but to pass through these risky 
zones to reach targeted locations to engage Maoists. 


To plan the operation, a joint meeting was held in the office of the SP. It was decided to launch the joint ops in the 
night of !7.07.206 under leadership of the SP Shrl Babu Ram who had decided to participate in the ops himself. A joint team 
consisting of District Police Force Aurangabad and four teams of cobra 205 battalion with their respective Commanders was 
constituted immediately under overall leadership of SP Babu Ram in order to apprehend the Maoists and to defy their nefarious 
plan. Two cobra teams No. | and |2 started from Madanpur at about 0:30 PM same day and after crossing thick forests, 
treacherous trenches and flooded rivers on foot in rainy dark night reached the destination at about 06:30 AM. The other two 
teams l4 & |8 also reached at their positions. Despite a long and constant ambush and search, no movement or trace of Maoists 
was found. Then all the teams moved one by one in east direction towards Dumri Nala. At about l:5 AM on 8-07-20I6 
when Shri Babu Ram with his two teams crossed the Dumri Nala, and the other two teams met a sudden series of Landmines 
blasts followed by a heavy gunshot fire. Some jawans had been Injured badly in the blast. At the same time, Maoists started 
firing on the team led by SP Babu Ram. He Immediately directed other team leader to take his team to help the teams stranded 
in Nala to evacuate and team led by SP Babu Ram retaliated to the enemy with a heavy fire to dominate them. 


Though engulfed in Incessant and iindiscriminate fire, SP Babu Ram along with his team, took tactical position 
immediately and motivated his Jawans to retaliate the enemy with full capacity. Under a strategic decision, the SP without 
caring for bullets of enemy and for his life and showing exemplary courage, maneuvered to the left side of the attack and told 
team member to attack the right flank. The Maoists, sensing that they were being commered, accelerated their firings to the full 
extent. CT Ravi Kumar crawled ahead, amidst the firing and fired upon enemy bravely when he got a bullet injury. The SP 
directed team member to take care of the injured Jawan and he himself along with his district force commandos advanced close 
to the enemy taking tactical cover of boulders and began to fire on the enemy from close distance. This gallant act resulted in 
breakage of Maoist ambush. CT Surendra Paswan, CT Vikas Kumar and CT Sanjay Kumar Choudhary exhibited exemplary 
courage. They without caring of the danger and firing from the enemy, kept advancing towards the enemy and fired on the 
enemy simultaneously. CT Surender Paswan despite being injured, advanced with the SP amid heavy firing. The enemy was 
in a dominating position and firing continuously. Our jawans being at lower position could not take aim at the top of the hill. 
Despite all odds and challenges the enemy was posing, the SP despite himself being injured, enthusing the morale of his Jawans, 
motivated them to capture the hill top without exposing themselves to the enemy's eye sight. Under the tactical leadership of 
the SP, the team taking covers of boulders succeeded in approaching the target, i.e. top of the hill under effective support of 
cover fire. The teams having approached the target, positioned themselves and discharged heavy fire on Maoists group under 
the command of SP Babu Ram. This befitting reply with utmost courage and absolute determination pushed the Maoists back 
and they fled away with their injured and killed members under the cover of IED blasts leaving three dead bodies of their 
accomplices, ammunitions in huge quantity, IEDs, explosives and other belongings. 


On other side, teams stranded in Nala also showed exemplary courage. Despite being injured SI Tarbabu Yadav and 
SI Ram Raj Singh in coordination with Cobra teams retaliated the enemy bravely and with strong determination in order to 
suppress their fire power, and evacuated the injured jawans regardless of the risk to their lives. This courageous counter attack 
by the police team broke the ambush laid by the Maoists and forced them to fled away. Uncaring for his personal safety, SI 
Saket Saurabh actively participated in the process of removal of injured Jawans to a safe place despite the attack made by the 
Maoists. 


In the face of prevailing apprehension of landmines laid by the Maoists on track along Dumri Nala, the only exit route 
and probability of presence of their armed groups on hilltop in way, the SP Aurangabad displaying remarkable professional 
skill with indomitable spirit and outstanding leadership evacuated seven injured jawans safely from the ambush site making 
cross country movement. 


In this operation, S/Shri Babu Ram, IPS, Superintendent of Police, Saket Saurabh, Sub-Inspector, Ram Raj Singh, 
Sub-Inspector, Tarbabu Yadav, Sub-Inspector, Sanjay Kumar Choudhary, Constable, Surendra Paswan, Constable and Vikas 
Kumar, Constable of Bihar Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 8/07/206.

No. 87—Pres/2025- The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Chhattisgarh Police:— 


Sl.No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
l. Sunil Sharma, IPS Superintendent of Police GM 
oH Sandeep Kumar Madile Sub-Inspector GM 
3. Madkam Pandu Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Bhejji Area of Sukma District is considered to be the stronghold of CPI (Maoists). Due to its geographical features 
with the presence of heavy dense forests, wide ranges of hills and a number of small streams, it turns to be the hiding zone for 
the Maoists. Inputs regarding the presence of 35-40 naxals in the Danteshpuram area of PS Bhejji is regularly received with 
this background. 


On receiving specific inputs from reliable sources, an anti-naxal operation was launched at around 8.0 hrs. on 
07.05.2023 from PS Errabore under the leadership of Shri Sunil Sharma, IPS, SP, Sukma. In order to ensure the proper 
execution of the plan Shri Sunil Sharma, SP Sukma himself briefed the operation party regarding die intelligence inputs and 
the proper execution of the plan. This team after proper briefing, along with requisite arms and ammunitions and 
communication equipment, headed towards villages of Pantabhejji, Mailasur and Danteshpuram. 


Meanwhile, the team commander Shri Sunil Sharma, as per the plan, launched the supporting parties. The first team 
of 202 CoBRA Bn marched from Kolaiguda to Village Gachchanpalli. The second party of 50th Bn of CRPF marched from 
Elarmadgu to village Mailasur-Bhodrajpadar and the third party being of 2l9th Bn of CRPF marched from Kottacheru to 
village Bhandarpadar. 


The operation route was through the villages of Pantabhejji, Mailasur and Danteshpuram. After reaching the target 
area and accessing the scenario, Shri Sunil Sharma gave further instruction to the team and encouraged the party to move 
forward. At around 05:30 hrs. in the morning, the party taking proper cover started returning along cross-country route, 
suddenly an indiscriminate firing was opened upon the police personnel by around 35-40 naxal cadres, wearing black uniform 
and ununifonned dress of banned PLGA Battalion. The enemy party had sophisticated automatic weapons and equipments. 
The aim of this attack was to kill police personnel and to loot their weapons and ammunitions. The Security personnel took 
position and warned the naxals to stop the firing and urged them to surrender. However, in response to the pleas to surrender; 
the naxals started attacking even more vigorously. Consequently, the police party had no other option but to reciprocate in self 
defence. During the course of this attack, the team leader Shri Sunil Sharma, marching tactically forward, directed one team 
member to cordon from the right direction and directed DRG Commander SI Sandeep Kumar Madile to cordon from the left 
direction. Shri Sunil Sharma with his team headed towards the naxals and tried to push back. All the parties moved forward, 
fire and started encircling the Maoists with exemplary courage and leading from the front. They kept on encouraging the police 
personnel to give fitting reply to the Maoists. Meanwhile naxals continued the attack with automatic weapons. 


Realizing the sudden aggression by the police party, the Maoists retreated taking the advantage of thick forest cover 
and nearby hillocks. After the fierce gun battle which lasted for around 35-40 minutes, the whole area was thoroughly searched 
and teams recovered 0] uniformed male body and 0! ununiformed female body along with one 303 rifle with magzing having 
one live bullet along with daily use items. The party also recovered 0! No. naxal made BGL Cell, 0l SLR magazine loaded 
with 8 live rounds. During the search operation in attack area, the security personnel recovered !0 blank rounds of AK-47 
rifle, 02 blank rounds of 303 rifle, 05 blank rounds of SLR rifle, 03 blank rounds of INSAS rifle and from the attacking spot of 
police party recovered blank rounds-2lof AK-47 rifle and 06 blank rounds of SLR rifle. 


During police naxal encounter, naxals fired around 200-300 rounds upon security personnel whereas security 
personnel fired 357 rounds from AK-47, |l8 rounds from SLR, 23 rounds from INSAS and along with 05 nos. of UBGL. 


In this operation, S/Shri Sunil Sharma, IPS, Superintendent of Police, Sandeep Kumar Madile, Sub-Inspector and 
Madkam Pandu, Constable of Chhattisgarh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher 
order.

personnel of Chhattisgarh Police:— 


Sl.No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
l. Madkam Hadma Constable GM 
2: Madkam Deva Constable GM 
3. Barse Hunga Constable GM

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


An anti-naxal operation was launched on |.08.2020 at around 2.5 hrs from Jagargunda PS under the leadership of 
SI Mukesh Patel. The Party launched from Jagargunda divided into two groups i.c DRG and CRPF. The operation route was 
through the villages Chimli, Nilgod, Mekalguda and Pulampad. After thoroughly searching the whole area, the party moved 
tactically towards the task. On |2.08.2020 at around 06.30 hrs while the party was marching in between the forest of Pulampad 
and Mekalguda, an indiscriminate firing was opened upon the DRG and DEF Police personnel by around 40-50 naxal cadres, 
wearing black uniforms and ununiformed dress of banned PLGA Battalion bearing sophisticated automatic weapons and 
equipment. This attack aimed to kill police personnel and loot their weapons and ammunition. The Security personnel took 
position and warned the naxals to stop the firing and urged them to surrender. However in response to pleas to surrender, the 
naxals started attacking even more vigorously. Consequently, the police party had no other option but to reciprocate in self- 
defense. During this attack, the team leader SI Mukesh Patel marching tactically forward, communicated with DRG 
Commander Nanda Markam and directed him to cordon from the right direction and directed another DRG Commander Umesh 
Markam to cordon from the left direction. SI Mukesh Patel along with his team headed towards the naxals and tried to push 
them back. All the parties moved forward and retaliated tactically to the naxal attack. Meanwhile, naxals continued the attack 
with automatic weapons and used BGL upon the Security personnel. 


The Security personnel followed the naxals for about I.5 km to 2.0 km. Seeing the aggressive approach of security 
personnel, the naxals ran away into the jungle. Aftermath when the security personnel launched the Cordan and search 
operation, the naxals executed a surprise attack upon them. In response, the security forces took position and retaliated. Due to 
the quick response by SI Mukesh Patel, Constable Madkam Hadma, Constable Madkam Deva, Constable Barse Hunga and 
Auxiliary Constable Roshan Gupta, naxals failed in the plan to break the ambush. Realizing the aggression by the police party, 
the Maoists retreated taking advantage of thick forest cover and nearby hillocks. 


After the fierce gun battle which lasted for around 02 hours, the whole area was thoroughly searched and police team 
recovered 02 uniformed male bodies and 02 ununiformed male bodies along with 0l No. 303 rifles (04 nos. live bullets), 03 
nos. Bharmar rifle, 0! desi bomb along with daily used items. During the search operation in the attack area, the security 
personnel recovered 05 blank rounds of AK-47, 04 blank rounds of SLR rifle, 03 blank rounds of Insas rifle and 06 blank 
rounds of 303 rifle. 


During the Police naxal encounter, naxals fired around 450-500 rounds upon security personnel whereas security 
personnel fired 9]8 rounds along with 0 HE bomb and 03 Nos. of UBGL. 

Later the dead naxal were identified as Bhogam Bhima S/o Bhogam 0५9, 30 years old (member of platoon no 0), 
Madkam Bhima S/o Late Madkam Ayta, 28 years old (Militia coy member), Oyam Joga S/o Oyam Nanda, 25 years old (Militia 
coy member) and Nuppo Deva S/o Nuppo Bhima, 28 years old (Militia coy member). 

In this operation, S/Shri Madkam Hadma, Constable, Madkam Deva, Constable, Barse Hunga, Constable and Roshan 
Gupta, Auxiliary Constable of Chhattisgarh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher 
order. 

The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 2/08/2020.

No. 89—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM), posthumously to the under 
mentioned personnel of Chhattisgarh Police:— 


Sl. No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
l. Late Ramuram Nag Assistant Sub-Inspector GM (Posthumous) 
2. Late Kunjam Joga Constable GM (Posthumous) 
3. Late Vanjam Bhima Constable GM (Posthumous) 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


In pursuance of SP office Dated 24-02-2023, an operation was launched from Jagargunda. The team along with 
requisite arms and ammunitions and communication equipment let off the operation. 


The operation route was through the villages Kamapara, Singaram, chimlipenta after thoroughly searching the whole 
area, party moved tactically towards the task. At around 0835 hrs, while party was marching towards Jagargunda Ashram para 
towards Singaram, near T Point, an indiscriminate firing was opened upon the front party ie DRG Chintagufa 2 and DRG 
Burkapal 2 by around 300-400 naxal cadres of banned PLGA Battalion bearing sophisticated automatic weapons and 
equipments. The aim of this attack was to kill police personnel and to loot their weapons and ammunitions. The naxal attacked 
from all directions and also fired from motar shells upon the police party. The security personnel took position and warned the 
naxals to stop the firing and urge them to surrender. However in response to pleas to surrender, the naxals started attacking 
even more vigorously. Consequently, the police party had no other option but to fire in self defence. During the course of this 
attack, Commander of DRG Burkapal 2 and Commander of DRG Chintagufa along with their team communicated with other 
back parties about the firing. The other team forwarded towards the EOF area by encircling the area from right direction and 
at the same time team of ASI Nanda Madkam with TI Shankar Lal Dhruw encircled from left direction. Both the parties 
tactically moved forward while firing the naxals. DRG Burkapal 2 and Chintagufa 2 was being fired upon by naxal from three 
different directions. All the parties were retaliating to the naxal attack. Meanwhile naxals continued the attack with automatic 
weapons and used BGL upon the Security personnel. 


During the firing, all members and officers of the aforesaid teams coordinated and bravely defended their positions in 
the face of heavy naxal attack and overwhelming odds and numerical superiority. In order to beat the ambush, DRG Chintagufa 
2 and DRG Burkapal 2 bravely attacked and during this Commander ASI Ramuram Nag, Constable Kunjam Joga and Constable 
Vanjam Bhima were seriously injured by bullet and splinters. The injured personnel, during the injury kept their momentum 
high and due to their gallant action the other jawans were saved and able to counter the ambush. During the attack, members 
from Security personnel kept moving ahead which forced the naxal to retreat. During this EOF, near about 7-8 naxals were 
seen injured who were taken naxalites with them. The injured police personnel facing the intense firing situation for the sake 
of motherland, at last martyred. The EOF lasted for around 40-45 min. and naxals fired about 000- 200 rounds and 205-300 
BGL. The Security personnel on defence fired 363 rounds and 35 Shells,!2 No HE Bomb, 05 nos. UBGL. During the course 
of firing, naxal looted the arms and ammunition of two martyrs. Due to the quick response by teams, naxals fail to loot the 
arms and ammunitions of one martyr. 


The martyred Police Personnel showed utter disregard to personnel safety and wellbeing while they had the option to 
retreat to higher ground. The martyrs laid down their lives and kept firing despite the numerical superiorly of the enemy and in 
face of death. Blood spots and tail signs of bodies being dragged were also found on various locations. During the aforesaid 
attack, surrendered naxal cadres present in the police party identified some naxal leaders in the attack as Hidma- battalion 
commander, Sonu- PLGA company no. | commander, Nagesh- PLGA company no. 2 commander, Jaiman madvi, Sannu, 
Madkam raghu, Sanvati, Anil, Dula, Ratan, Rajesh, Masa (all PLGA platoon commanders). 


In this operation, S/Shri Late Ramuram Nag, Assistant Sub-Inspector, Late Kunjam Joga, Constable and Late Vanjam 
Bhima, Constable of Chhattisgarh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 25/02/2023.

2 Madvi Sannu Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On the I9th April 202, SP Dantewada received precise human intelligence regarding the presence of banned CPI 
(Maoists) including the dreaded presence of 08-!0 men and women Maoists, Maoist Malangir Area Committee members 
Santosh Kosa Gonche and Lakhma Korram in the forest of Nilavaya Mailapara under the Aranpur Police Station. Nilawaya 
Mallapara is one of the highly sensitive Naxal-affected villages and a core area of banned CPI (Maoists) activities. 


Accordingly, a detailed operational plan was formulated and the DRG party total strength 4] forces, advanced at 2:55 
pm under the command of Inspector Santosh Hernia. As the Police party reached the forest area of Nilawaya Mailapara, 
Naxalites who had laid an ambush suddenly started firing indiscriminately on the police forces. The DRG troop, under the 
command of Inspector Santosh Hernia, retaliated strongly. After some time, the firing stopped and a detailed search operation 
was conducted in the exchange of fire area. 


During the search operation, the body of a male Maoist was recovered and later identified as Kosa alias Gonche, the 
Malangir Area Committee Member/MI Incharge, son of Handa resident of Nilavaya Mallapara, PS Aranpur, District 
Dantewada and 0] pistol with a magazine, 0| live round, 0] blank cartridge, 0! Bharmar gun, 0 tiffin bomb, 0! black uniform, 
09 bamboo spikes and |0 tiger bombs were recovered from the exchange of fire site. 


The role Constable Suraj Kumar Markam and Constable Madvi Sannu was crucial in the success of the security forces. 
This operational success in the den of Maoists pushed them back and helped the police to gain a strong foothold in the area. 


In this operation, S/Shri Suraj Kumar Markam, Constable and Madvi Sannu, Constable of Chhattisgarh Police 
displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023.

No. 9l—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Chhattisgarh Police:— 


Sl.No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
Karod Singh Company Commander GM 
2. Purushottam Dewangan Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 3th January 202, precise human intelligence was received by SP Dantewada regarding the presence of banned 
CPI (Maoists) which included the dreaded presence of 07-08 Maoists along with Katekalyan Area Committee's LGS 
Commander Budhra Sodhi, Area Committee, members Mahngu, Hidma Muchaki, Budhram Markam, Madda and Muya in the 
forest area of Chikpal, Marjum, Bhimapara, Hurrapara under the Katekalyan Police Station is one of the highly sensitive Naxal- 
affected villages and a core area of banned CPI (Maoists) activities. A detailed operational plan was made and the CAF and 
DRG party (Total strength 46 forces) advanced at 03:00 under the command of CC Karod Singh, !7 BN, CAF Camp Chikpal. 


As the police party reached the forest area of Bhimapara and Hurrapara between Chikpal and Marjum, Naxalites who 
had laid down an ambush suddenly started firing indiscriminately on the police forces. The DRG troop under the command of 
Company Commander Shri Karod Singh retaliated strongly. After some time, the firing stopped and a detailed search operation 
was conducted in the exchange of fire area. In which the body of a male Maoist was recovered by later identified as Katekalyan 
Area Committee Member and Jantana Sarkar Raksha Sakha sachiv (Entire Katekalyan Area Committee) Hidma Muchaki, son 
of Sannu Muchaki, Village Chikpal, PS Katekalyan Distt. Dantewada. Also 0 Pistol with a magazine, 0] tiffin bomb, electrical 
wire 05 meters, 02 Nos. bomb detonator, Naxalite uniform and other useful daily items were recovered from the incident site. 


The role of Company Commander Shri Karod Singh and DRG Constable Purushottam Dewangan were crucial in the 
success of the security forces success. This operational success in the den of Maoists pushed them back and helped the police 
to gain a strong foothold in the area. 


In this operation, S/Shri Karod Singh, Company Commander and Purushottam Dewangan, Constable of Chhattisgarh 
Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

No. 92—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


SI. No. | Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
l. Nazir Ahmad Wani Head Constable GM 
2. Nisar Maqbool Head Constable GM 
3. Mohammad Iqbal Mir Constable GM 
4. Dawood Ahmad Mir Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


A group of proscribed terror outfit of "JeM” planned to carry out fidayeen attack on Police/Security forces and to 
target minorities, migrant laboureres withing the JD of District Kulgam under the command and control of Shahid @ Shahzad 
R/o Pakistan in association with Uzair Ahmad Bhat S/o Mohd Maqbool Bhat R/o Mirhama and Mohd Shafi Dar S/o Gh. Hassan 
Dar R/o Wagad Tral. However, Kulgam Police made efficient use of human as well as technical resources to foil their nefarious 
designs in advance and to ensure that they will not get any chance to carry out any terror crime within District Kulgam and to 
neutralize them on priority. 


On 29.2.202, Kulgam Police generated a credible input regarding presence of terrorists in village Mirhama Kulgam. 
Instantly SSP G.V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, IPS rushed to the suspected village alongwilh his QRTs, 9 RR & troops of CRPF 
8th, 46th, 63rd Bns and Range QAT CRPF Anantnag to cordon off the whole village to ensure that the trapped terrorists did 
not get any chance to flee trom the spot. 


As the village was cordoned off on war fooling basis, SSP Kulgam gave special priority to save the lives of civilians 
by evacuating them from the cordoned area towards safer places. After ensuring safety and security of civilians, a search party 
headed by SSP Kulgam leads towards the suspected house. The hiding terrorists indiscriminately fired upon the search party 
with the intention to kill them and run away. On this, SSP Kulgam alongwith his team retaliated & engaged in fierce combat 
with terrorists leading to an encounter. During gun battle, brave officer with his QRTs alongwith 9 RR & troops of GRPF |8, 
46, 63 Battalions, Range QATCRPP Anantnag neutralized three hardcore categorized terrorists on spot without any collateral 
damage and putting their lives and limbs in risk in the interest of security of state and national integrity. 


The coherence shown by Police party played an exemplary role in elimination of three dreaded terrorists without 
caring about safety of their personal lives. The eliminated terrorists were identified as Shahid @ Shahzad R/o Pakistan of JEM 
terror outfit, Uzair Ahmed Bhat S/o Mohd Magbool Blial R/o Mirhama Kulgam of HM terror outfit and Mohd Shafi Dar S/o 
Gh. Hassan Dar R/o Wagad Tral of JeM terror outfit. Arms & ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain 
terrorists. Case FIR U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A. Act, 3, 6, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Kulgam for further 
investigation. 

Death of these terrorist was a big jolt to structural and functional unit of banned terrorist organization “JeM”. These 
terrorists were also involved in motivating youth to join terrorist ranks. Their objective was to vitiate peace and create a fueling 
of chaos among the pro-democratic people, thus injuring and hurting the basic sentinel of democracy. 

The striking gallant act on the part of HC Nazir Ahmad Wani, HC Nisar Maqbool, Ct Mohammad Iqbal Mir and Ct 
Dawood Ahmad Mir faced a volley of burst fire by terrorists and miraculously escaped from the bullets and grenades lobbed 
on them. 


In this operation, S/Shri Nazir Ahmad Wani, Head Constable, Nisar Maqbool, Head Constable, Mohammad Iqbal 
Mir, Constable and Dawood Ahmad Mir, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and 
devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 29/2/202].

No. 93—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) and Ist Bar to GM to the 
under mentioned personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
l. Mohd Sadeeq Assistant Sub Inspector GM 
2. Maajid Afzal Wani Head Constable lst Bar to GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 9.06.2022, Kulgam Police generated a credible input regarding the presence of terrorists in village Dhanipora 
Gujjarpora. Instantly, SSP G. V. Sundeep Ghakravarthy, IPS rushed towards the suspected village alongwith his QRTs, 9 RR 
& CRPF |8th Bn. to cordon off the whole village and to ensure that the trapped terrorists may not get any chance to flee from 
the spot. On laying initial cordon and confirming the fact that terrorists are hiding in dense apple orchard, it was very difficult 
to identify the target area as it was surrounded by densely populated apple tree orchards. The cordon was further strengthened 
around the target area and search was started. During search, the area was zeroed in and the terrorists were given opportunity 
to surrender but the hiding terrorists fired indiscriminately upon the SFs/Police with the intention to kill them and make their 
escape good. 


As the encounter started, SSP Kulgam gave special priority to save the lives of civilians by evacuating them from the 
cordoned area towards safer places to ensure their safety and security. SSP Kulgam constituted different teams alongwith 
respective QRTs of Police & SFs to conduct the operation successfully. The first team headed by SSP Kulgam & team 
alongwith 09 RR .and contingents of CRPF |8dl Bn who cordoned off the target area from Northern and Western side while 
the 2nd team headed by DySP Shabir Ahmad, ASI Mohd Sadeeq and team alongwith troops of 09 RR/CRPF |8th Bn who 
cordoned off the target area from Southern and Eastern side. After analyzing the situation, the teams as per operational plan 
moved towards target location from all sides. Both the teams were monitoring the movements of the terrorists very keenly & 
traced their movement within shortest possible time & engaged in fierce combat with the terrorists. During ongoing gun battle, 
the lst party gunned down 0] terrorist on spot. 


However, the 2nd terrorist was continuously changing his position by lobbing hand grenades and heavy volume of 
firing towards security forces and tried to break the cordon to manage flee from the cordoned area. However, the 2nd party 
which showed presence of mind & alertness; challenged the terrorist in open & gunned down him without any collateral 
damage. The brave act on part of ASI Mohd Sadeeq and HC Maajid Afzal Wani was highly commendable keeping in view the 
circumstances as well as heavy exchange of firing between terrorists and security forces. The synergetic professionalism shown 
by District Police Kulgam, troops Of 9 RR & CRPF |8th Bn under these circumstances is indispensable to mark end to terrorism 
& to ensure safety and security of our country from enemies who are hell bent to recruit the local youth into terrorist ranks 
thereby disturbing the peaceful environment and to target the minorities, PRIs, non-local labourers as well as political activists. 


The coherence shown by the aforesaid Police party in elimination of 02 dreaded terrorists without caring for their 
personal lives was commendable. The eliminated terrorists were later on identified as Haris Sharief Rather S/o Sharief Din 
Rather R/o Zafran Colony Ichnambal Pantha chowk Srinagar and Zakir Ahmad Padder S/o Ghulam Ahmad Padder R/O 
Mirhama Kulgam. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 54/2022 U/S 307 
IPC, 7/2 I.A.Act, 6, 8, 9, 20, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S D.H.Pora for further investigation. 


In this operation, S/Shri Mohd Sadeeq, Assistant Sub Inspector and Maajid Afzal Wani, Head Constable of Jammu & 
Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 9/06/2022. 


(File No. 020/74/2024-PMA)

702 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On .06.2022, following a credible input about the presence of terrorists, Police Kulgam in assistance with Army 9 
RR & l8th Bn. CRPF; launched a joint operation in village Khandipora, Kulgam. On laying initial cordon and confirming the 
fact that terrorists are hiding in the target house, the cordon was further strengthened and search was started. During search, the 
target house was zeroed in and terrorists were given opportunity to surrender but the hiding terrorists fired indiscriminately 
upon the initial searching party ensuing an encounter. 


As the encounter started, SSP G.V. Sundeep Chakravarthy gave special priority to save the precious lives of civilians 
by evacuating them from the cordoned area towards safer places to ensure their safety and security as the area was very 
congested. After evacuation of trapped civilians, SSP Kulgam constituted two teams to conduct the operation without any 
collateral damage and to ensure implementation of operational SOPs on ground. The Ist team headed by DySP Gh. Mohd Bhat 
and the 2nd team was from the back side of the target house headed by DySP Shabir Ahmad Khan. The teams as per operational 
plan moved towards target location from all sides and got engaged in fierce combat with terrorists. During on-going gun battle, 
both the operational parties gunned down one terrorist without any collateral damage. The eliminated terrorist was later-on 
identified as Rasiq Ahmad Ganie S/o Mohd Amin Ganie R/o Souch Kulgam of HM outfit. The brave act on part of DySP 
Shabir Ahmad Khan and HC Manzoor Ahmad Malik was highly commendable keeping in view the circumstances as well as 
heavy exchange of fire between terrorists and security forces. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain 
terrorist. Case FIR No. 67/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 3, 8, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Kulgam for 
further investigation. 


The officers/officials of Kulgam Police, Army 9 RR and troops of CRPF |8th Bn and their respective teams ensured 
safety of lives and properties of civilians during this successful operation. The professional acumen of the officers resulted in 
a swift and clean operation. Such a swift and clean operation gave psychological blow to anti-national elements, their 
propaganda machinery and at the same time provided motivation to security forces engaged in fighting terrorism in the area. 


In this operation, S/Shri Shabir Ahmad Khan, Deputy Superintendent of Police and Manzoor Ahmad Malik, Head 
Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from /06/2022.

No. 95—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

l. Bilal Ahmad Head Constable GM 

2 Karan Singh Head Constable GM 

3: Sami- Ullah Shah Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 07.06.2022, at about l500 hrs, Shopian Police received an intelligence input about the presence of terrorists in 
orchard area of village Aloora, Shopian. On this, Police Shopian launched an operation in collaboration with 34 RR, |78th BN 
CRPF. While establishing initial cordon, it was decided that civilians who were inside the village should be safely taken out 
from the inner cordon without getting panic or situation gets worsened. 


The team under the overall supervision of SSP Tanushree alongwith DySP Mohd Ashrif, HC Javid Ahmad, HC 
Gayour Ul Haq, HC Bilal Ahmad, HC Karan Singh, SgCt Ishtiaq Ahmad, Const. Sami-Ullah Shah, SPO Sadam Hussain, SPO 
Bashir Ahmad, SPO Umer Mushtaq and team of 34 RR & |78th BN CRPF were formed to start the search of target area. A 
team of |78th BN CRPF was deputed at village DK-Pora-Aloora and Shopian Police was deputed at village Sehpora/Dairpora- 
Aloora. Maximum orchard area was cordon off quickly and after getting confirmation of hiding terrorists in the orchard area 
of the said village. 


Initially some houses and a bit area adjoining to the target area were brought under the cordon tactfully by initial joint 
cordon team under the overall supervision of SSP Shopian. The one team started to combing the orchard from South-West and 
other team of DySP Raiz Ahmed including HC Bilal Ahmad, HC Ravi Kumar, SgCt. Mushtaq Ahmad, Const. Sami-Ullah 
Shah, Const. Mohmad Hussain, Const. Vicky Khajuria were combing the orchard from North & South-East sides respectively. 


PART [-.86०८. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 703 


During search 03-04 strangers were spotted and challenged them to ask their identity but they opened firing indiscriminately 
on search party to stop their advance as well as in an attempt to break the cordon and make their escape good. After this 
deliberate fire, terrorists ran and jumped into nearby Nala. However, DySP Mohd Ashrif, HC Javid Ahmad, HC Gayour Ul 
Haq, HC Jatinder Singh, HC Karan Singh, SgCt. Ishtiaq Ahmad, SPO Saddam Hussain, SPO Bashir Ahmad and SPO Umer 
Mushtaq fired a few rounds in retaliation which were followed by the other joint teams. 


In the meantime, search was re-initiated by the searching party DySP Raiz Ahmed alongwith HC Bilal Ahmad, HC 
Ravi Kumar, SgCt. Mustaq Ahmad, Const. Sami-Ullah Shah, Const. Mohmad Hussain, Const. Vicky Khajuria.During the 
search operation, target area was pinpointed/zeroed by the search teams. When the troops were advancing, terrorists hiding in 
orchard area sensing the movement of operational parties opened indiscriminate fire upon the search parties. The search parties 
immediately took cover and retaliated with courage triggering a fierce gunfight between the terrorists and search parties. Police 
teams headed by DySP Raiz Ahmed including HC Bilal Ahmad, HC Ravi Kumar, SgCt. Mushtaq Ahmad, Ct. Sami-Ullah, Ct. 
Mohmad Hussain, Ct. Vicky Khajuria, putting their lives at a great risk and advanced while firing with utmost precautions. 
During their advancement, they saw that a terrorist was hiding inside the Nala with the cover of edge and firing indiscriminately 
on Police/SFs. However, joint cordoned team tactfully encircled the terrorist and challenged him to stop his firing and asked 
for surrender. But instead of surrender, he kept firing on the joint team of Police/SFs. Team of joint forces retaliated in the 
exchange of fire due to which hiding terrorist got injured. In a bid to inflict damage to the security forces, he stood up and 
opened fire indiscriminately on inner cordon team. As the terrorist was exposed, DySP Mohd Ashrif, HC Javid Ahmad, HC 
Gayour UI Haq, HC Jatinder Singh, HC Karan Singh, SgCt Ishtiaq Ahmad, SPO Saddam Hussain, SPO Bashir Ahmad and 
SPO Umer Mushtaq opened the barrel of their AK Rifle in a heavy volume. After a prolonged gun battle, a thorough search 
was done in which dead body of one killed terrorist along with his weapon was recovered in the Nalla and was later on identified 
as Raja Nadeem Rather S/o Abdul Rehman Rather R/O Ashmuji, District Kulgam of LeT terror outfit. Arms/ammunition 
recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 7/22 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 IAAct, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands 
registered in P/S Imamsahib. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by HC Bilal Ahmad, HC Karan Singh and Ct. 
Sami-ullah Shah who fought with terrorist efficiently/intelligently without caring for their lives remarkable as the endeavour 
to reach the target in extremely hostile situation was truly gallant. Although, the terrorist tried his best to cause damage to 
forces by resorting to indiscriminate firing in which they had a very narrow escape. 


In this operation, S/Shri Bilal Ahmad, Head Constable, Karan Singh, Head Constable and Sami- Ullah Shah, Constable 
of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 07/06/2022.

No. 96—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned

Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
Sheikh Abid Sub Inspector GM 

2: Ishfaq Ahmad Wani Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 28.05.2022, a general input about the presence of terrorists in village Sheetipora Bijbehara was received from 
reliable sources. The input was further developed and presence of terrorists was confirmed in the house of one Abdul Rashid 
Bhat S/o Assadullah Bhat R/o Sheetipora. Acting on the information, a joint strategy was chalked out by Police Anantnag in 
collaboration with 3rd RR and CRPF 40/90 Bns. Accordingly heavy deployment of SOG Anantnag with active assistance from 
the troops of 3 RR, and 40/90 Bn CRPF was launched for a counter insurgency operation under the command of SSP Anantnag 
and effective supervision of SP (Ops) Anantnag and other Police Officers of District Anantnag present on the ground to control 
and coordinate the sensitive operation. It was decided to cordon the said area for which proper strategy was chalked out to 
encircle the hiding terrorists thereby not giving them any chance to break the cordon as the said terrorists had earlier managed 
to break the cordon, successfully even after inflicting casualties on Police/SFs many times in the past. At the first instance, 
cordon around the suspected area was tightened /strengthened and the civilians were evacuated on priority out of the house/ 
cordoned area to avoid any civilian causality. The whole cordoned area was sealed & proper security arrangements were made 
for successful operation under the overall command of SSP Anantnag. Proper deployment was made at strategic points to 


704 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


prevent escape of the hiding terrorists and search of the identified was house was launched. During search operation presence 
of terrorists was confirmed in the said house. 


After analyzing the, situation, SSP Anantnag alerted the troops to keep the confidence & hold the cordon strongly, in 
order to make the operation successful two teams, Ist under the command of SSP Anantnag and 2nd under the command of 
SP(Ops) Anantnag including SI Sheikh Abid & HC Ishfaq Ahmad Wani and personnel of 3rd RR/QRTs of CRPF were 
constituted. Before launching final assault, the hiding terrorists were asked to lay down their arms but there was no response 
from terrorists’ side. Accordingly, a search party advanced towards the house to get entry but terrorists who were highly trained; 
showered volley of bullets towards the party in which they had a narrow escape. The terrorists’ fire was effectively retaliated 
by the Police party which ended into elimination of two terrorists who were later on identified as Yawar Ayoub Dar S/o 
Mohammad Ayoub Dar R/o Dogripora Awantipora (Category “C” of HM outfit) and Ishfaq Ahmad Ganie S/o Farooq Ahmad 
Ganie R/o Shalgam Srigufwara (Category “B” of HM outfit). Arms/Ammunition was recovered from the possession of 
eliminated terrorists. Case FIR No. 82/2022 U/S 307 IPG 7/25 I A Act 6,9,20, ULAP Act stands registered in Police Station 
Bijbehara for further investigation. 


In this operation, S/Shri Sheikh Abid, Sub Inspector and Ishfaq Ahmad Wani, Head Constable of Jammu & Kashmir 
Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. 024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 28/05/2022.

No. 97—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) and Ist Bar to GM 
(posthumously) to the under mentioned personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
Late Himayun Muzzamil Deputy Superintendent of Police lst Bar to GM (Posthumous) 
2. | Swarn Singh SgCT GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 27.05.2022, a specific input was received from reliable sources regarding presence/ movement of unknown 
terrorist(s) carrying illegal arms/ ammunition in Ellahi Bagh (Shah Faisal Lane) area of Soura Srinagar, falling within 
jurisdiction of P/S Soura, Srinagar. It was also learnt that the terrorist(s) have also plans to carry-out terror activity in the area. 
Immediately acting on the tip-off, the area was cordoned-off by the joint police parties of PC Srinagar and Police Srinagar 
consisting of nafri of Hazratbal Zone and Escort/Column nafri of SSP Srinagar for searches under the overall supervision of 
then SSP Srinagar to track-down the hiding terrorist(s) after proper identification and exact location so that appropriate action, 
as warranted is taken without any collateral damage. While the searches were being conducted, terrorist(s) hiding in the locality 
in an under-construction house, opened indiscriminate fire upon the searching parties with the intention to kill them and manage 
their escape from the locality. 


After having confirmation regarding presence of terrorist(s) in the locality, immediately an operational plan was jointly 
chalked out by SSP Rakesh Balwal, IPS and SP Iftkhar Talib. Accordingly, 02 special teams comprising of DySP Mohan Lal, 
DySP Himayun Muzzammil, Inspr. Syed Iqbal Rashid, PSI Shafqat Hussain, SgCt. Rakesh Kumar, SgCt. Mohd Mubarak and 
Const. Varun Singh and HC Mohd Ashraf Wagay, SgCt. Fayaz Ahmad and others was tasked/assigned to eliminate terrorists 
by advancing from the front side of the target location and taking every precaution to avoid any collateral damage and 
evacuation of civilians, if any in the area to nearby safer places. 


Likewise, 2nd team comprising of DySP Sumit Sharma, HC Basharat Rasool, SgCt. Ayaz Ahmad, SgCt. Swarn Singh, 
SgCt. Mohd Iqbal Khan and Const. Tariq Ahmad and others was tasked/assigned to approach and attack the target location on 
rear side. 


All the nafri positioned themselves according to the plan, as enumerated above, encircling the identified target location 
(under-construction house). The intervention party having taken all precautions started approaching; however, as the 
intervention party reached near the target structure, it came under fire from the terrorists who had taken position inside the 
under-construction house. The holed-up terrorists lobbed grenades and fired towards the advancing party and the intervention 
party had a miraculous escape prompting the advance parties for final assault to neutralize the hiding terrorists. The advance 
parties approached the target location from all sides and during the gun fight between the operational parties, 02 terrorists were 
neutralized. 


PART [-.86०८. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 705 


Later on, the dead bodies of the killed terrorists were recovered from the scene of encounter and their identity were 
ascertained as local terrorists namely Afreen Altaf Malik @ Afridi Altaf Malik S/o Mohammad Altaf Malik R/o Baba khardar 
Trenz Imamsahab District Shopian and Shakir Ahmad Waza S/o Gulzar Ahmad Waza R/o Trenz Imamsahib District Shopian, 
both C-category of LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 5/2022 
U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6, l8, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Soura for further investigation. 


The operation was concluded without any collateral damage and elimination of the terrorists is a big jolt to the LeT 
terrorist network in South/ Central Kashmir Ranges. The role and the leadership shown by the operational parties remained 
exemplary. They also played an exceptional role in the coordination between the parties of Police from the beginning till the 
culmination of operation. They remained instrumental in the said operation by way of operational tactics viz evacuation of 
nearby civilians, chalking out best possible operational plan, priority on the lives of operational parties and assaulting on 
terrorists without any collateral damage. 


In this operation, S/Shri Late Himayun Muzzamil, Deputy Superintendent of Police and Swarn Singh, SgCT of Jammu 
& Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 27/05/2022.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 3].03,.2022 at about 2035 hours, Shopian Police received specific information regarding presence of terrorists of 
proscribed LeT outfit in village Turkawangam Shopian who were planning to carryout terror activities in the area. On this 
input, Shopian Police assisted with Army 44 RR/CRPF [78 Bn. cordoned off the area and started search operation under proper 
strategic plan to nab/eliminate the hiding terrorists. It was difficult to locate the target area in view of dense orchards and Nallas. 
Then, it was decided by the operational commander Addl. SP Ifroz Ahmad to take more area under cordon and all routes of 
escape were plugged off properly. In order to flush out the hiding terrorists, two joint assault groups of RR, CRPF & local 
Police were constituted Ist party led by DySP Riaz Ahmed and the 2nd party led by DySP Mohd Ashrif in association with 44 
RR & 78 BN CRPF. 


Soon the assault parties started combing the adjacent orchards to pinpoint the target area, the hiding terrorists on seeing 
the movement of operational parties started indiscriminate firing on them in which the assault parties had a very narrow escape. 
However, the operational parties took position quickly and retaliated the fire of terrorists effectively. Terrorists were asked to 
surrender, which they declined and instead resorted to grenade lobbing. However, operational men showed patience and did 
not retaliate the fire, owing to the fact that some civilians were trapped in the cordoned area. For evacuation of the trapped 
civilians, a well-planned strategy was chalked out and the team headed by DySP Riaz Ahmed under the covering support given 
by the other team led by DySP Mohd Ashrif in several attempts succeeded in evacuation of all the civilians trapped in the 
cordoned area. 


After evacuation process, the teams headed by DySP Riaz Ahmed and DySP Mohd Ashrif moved towards target to 
take the operation to its logical conclusion. However, holed-up terrorists were constantly changing their position taking 
advantage of the densely build-up with huge network of narrow lanes and were firing from all corners. Since, it was difficult 
to pinpoint exact location of terrorists, however advance party led by DySP Riaz Ahmed engaged terrorists and other hand 
troops of 44 RR & CRPF [7870 Bn also moved swiftly to take positions at the natural cover dense build-up. During these 
developments on noticing instant activity of holed-up terrorists, DySP Mohd Ashrif and his team exhibited extraordinary 
bravery and crawled towards the target in order to diminish the movement of terrorists. The terrorists observing movement of 
officers, hurled a hand grenade towards them, but the assault parties kept their nerves cool and managed to maintain their 
position and incessantly engaged terrorists at one place and did not provide them further chance to fire or lob grenade by 
showering bullets heavily in a close-range gunfight which resulted in elimination of hardcore terrorist, later on identified as 
Muneeb Ahmad Shiekh @ Muni S/o Shah Mohammad Sheikh R/o Tak-Mohalla Shopian affiliated with LeT terror outfit. 


706 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


Arms/ ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 26/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 IAAct 
6, 8, 20, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Zainapora for further investigation. 


The elimination of this hardcore terrorist of proscribed LeT outfit is a great achievement for J&K Police and on the 
other hand a big jolt for the LeT terrorist outfit. It is assessed that with the elimination of the terrorist there is considerable 
improvement in overall situation expected. The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by HC Ab. 
Majid & SgCt. Deepak Kumar who fought with terrorist efficiently and intelligently without caring for their precious lives, 
was remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. They evacuated the 
civilian trapped in the cordoned area thus evading civil causalities by presence of mind and good policing. 


In this operation, S/Shri Ab. Majid War, Head Constable and Deepak Kumar, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police 
displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 3/03/2022.

No. 99—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 
Manoj Kumar Deputy Superintendent of Police GM 

2. Mushtaq Ahmad Theekri Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 22.0.2022 at about 095 hours, Shopian Police received a credible input about presence of some terrorists in 
village Kilbal of District Shopian. As per input, terrorists were in process to carry out major terror activities in the area to 
threaten the general public. The said village which had been very vulnerable from terrorism point of view due to its topography, 
dense orchards and support base to terrorists and conducting an operation in such an area was a daunting task for the 
Police/Security forces. On this, Police Shopian launched a joint operation with District Police Ganderbal in collaboration with 
34 RR, |78th BN CRPF and while establishing initial cordon, it was decided that civilians who were inside the village should 
be safely taken out from the inner cordon without getting panic or situation gets worsen. 


With the same drill, team of Shopian/Ganderbal Police in collaboration with Army 34 RR & CRPF [78॥ Bn. under 
the overall supervision of SSP Amritpal Singh, IPS including SSP Nikhil Borkar, IPS, Addl. SP Ifroz Ahmad Mir, DySP Manoj 
Kumar, Inspr. Khurshid Ahmad, SgCt. Manzoor Ahmad, SgCt. Dilawar Ahmad, SgCt. Barkat Ali, SgCt. Aijaz Ahmad, Const. 
Ab. Rashid, Const. Irshad Ahmad, Const. Mushtaq Ahmad Theekri, Const. Rafiq Ahmad., SPO Parvez Ahmad, SPO Sajjad 
Ahmad, SPO Mohd Altaf, SPO Mudasir Ahmad and SPO Yash Paul alongwith Army 34 RR & CRPF |78th Bn. were formed 
to start the search of target area. While using professional skills and drills, the occupants of village Kilbal Shopian were asked 
about the presence of terrorists in their houses. 


However, all the occupants of the targeted houses initially denied the presence of terrorists in their houses. But during 
the spot interrogation of one occupant confirmed about the presence of two suspected terrorists in House No. 7 which was a 
double storey building and after getting confirmation of hiding terrorists in the targeted House No. |7 and to avoid any untoward 
incident, targeted house was covered from different directions. Initially some houses and a bit area adjoining to the target area 
were brought under the cordon tactfully by initial joint cordon team under the overall supervision of SSP Amritpal Singh. One 
team was formed to start the search of target area. During the search operation, target area was pinpointed/zeroed by the 
searching teams. 


In the meantime, terrorists hiding in the house sensing the movement of operational parties opened indiscriminate fire 
upon the search parties with their illegal automatic weapons with intention to kill them. The search parties immediately took 
cover and retaliated with courage triggering a fierce gunfight between the terrorists and search parties. However, it has been 
noticed that have got trapped in the cordoned area and they have been evacuated to safer place. 


Afterwards police teams headed by SSP Nikhil Borkar, IPS were formed for final assault to eliminate holed-up 
terrorists. With upmost control over firing, cordon teams kept on engaging the terrorists whenever their movement was noticed. 
During firing, the target house caught fire and holed-up terrorists tried to flee from House No. |7 which was clearly visible 
from house no. 22. Seeing this, Addl. SP Feroz Ahmad and his team fired on the terrorists in which terrorists got injured and 


PART [-.86९०. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 707 


hid inside the cowshed which also caught fire. In this condition, terrorists started indiscriminate firing all around to break the 
cordon of Police and Security forces. After some time, since the heavy firing was going on from the fire base (house no. 22) to 
the target cowshed, one terrorist who was equipped with AK weapon started targeting the team of DySP Mohd Ashrif. On this, 
Addl. SP Feroz Ahmad quickly ordered his team to go through crawling and take position from left door which was approx. 
20-25. 


The widows of house No. 22 were quite big and bullets of AK Rifle from terrorists were directly piercing parallel on 
the walls. Despite this deadly attack of terrorists; Addl. SP Feroz Ahmad ordered his team to fire together on the terrorists 
position and at the same time, all started firing in heavy volume compelling the hiding terrorists to come-out from the cowshed. 
As this firing was very heavy, terrorists in their last fight stood up in desperation and as soon as they disclosed their position 
in front of House No. 22; daredevil of SSP Nikhil Borkar, Addl. SP Feroz Ahmad including DySP Mohd Ashrif, Inspr. Khurshid 
Ahmad, SI Mohd Rafiq, ASI Abdul Hamid, Const. Hilal Ahmad, Const. Fareed Ahmad, SPO Manoj Kumar, SPO Ghulam 
Mustafa and SPO Iftikaar Ahmad rained the bullets from three sides on the terrorists and in the firing, both terrorists were 
neutralized. After a prolonged gun battle, though there was no firing from the target house and final search was ordered, still 
all precautions were made to avoid any last moment loss. Fire in the target house was extinguished and after cooling down of 
the temperature, a thorough search was done in which dead bodies of two killed terrorists along with their weapons were 
recovered. Later-on identification of eliminated terrorists was established as Rayees Ahmad Mir S/o Mohammad Ashraf Mir 
R/O Takiya Chinarbagh Pulwama and Sameer Ahmad Shah S/o Nazir Ahmad Shah R/o Dangam Shopian of LeT outfit. 
Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 06/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 
6, 8, 9 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Shopian for further investigation. 


In this operation, S/Shri Manoj Kumar, Deputy Superintendent of Police and Mushtaq Ahmad Theekri, Constable of 
Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 22/0/2022.

3. Rouf Ahmad Mir SgCT GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 9.2.202] at 0235 hours, a specific input was received from the human asset to PC Srinagar that some unknown 
terrorists in possession of illegal arms/ammunition are present in Darbagh Harwan area of District Srinagar and have plans to 
carry-out attacks on Police/ SFs in city Srinagar. In order to track down the terrorists after getting their exact location/ 
identification, Police teams of District Srinagar/ Police Component Srinagar alongwith CRPF (54 Bn) Valley QRT and nafri 
of Army (24 RR) were constituted to identify and neutralize terrorists without any collateral damage. The so-constituted teams 
subsequently started searches in the suspected area during which they succeeded in zeroing location where the terrorists were 
suspected to be hiding and the target location was encircled by the cordoned parties. During the initial course of Cordon and 
Search Operation, terrorists smelling the presence of Police parties opened indiscriminate firing and hurled grenades in order 
to make safe passage and fled away from the spot, but the Police/ SFs parties approached towards the target location from all 
sides besides plugged all the lanes. 


Immediately, an operational plan was chalked-out as per the operational SOPs and all the lanes and exit points 
approaching target location were sealed. The evacuation process of nearby houses was undertaken under the supervision of 
SP(PG) Srinagar. All the nafri positioned themselves according to the plan as enumerated above and the operational party very 
tactfully and bravely evacuated the inmates of the adjoining area and shifted them to a nearby safer place to avoid any collateral 
damage. The operational party having taken all precautions, started the process of advancing and during advancing, the hiding 
terrorist again fired indiscriminately towards the advancing party but the same didn't cause any damage owing to the tactical 
approach of the operational team thus had a miraculous escape. 


708 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


At this stage, SSP Rakesh Balwal, IPS being In charge of the operational parties constituted small team including of 
SP Tanushree, IPS, DySP Mohan Lal, DySP Surinder Singh, ASI Narinder Singh, ASI Bashir Ahmad, HG Sunil Kumar Koul, 
SgCt. Bilal Ahmad Ganie, SgCt. Rouf Ahmad Mir, SgCt. Javeed Ahmad Lone, SgCt. Yash Paul, SgCt. Aijaz Ahmad of PC 
Srinagar and SgCt. Shabir Ahmad Lone, SgCt. Fayaz Ahmad Mapnoo of District Srinagar alongwith adequate Nafri of QRT 
of 54 Bn CRPF, Valley OAT CRPF and personnel of Army (24 RR) to neutralize the terrorist without any collateral damage. 


The team so-constituted started the process of advancing and after reaching near the target while the effective cordon 
was placed by respective small teams from all sides. The targeted location was very challenging being built up area where 
compounds of most of the houses were separated from each other with high GC-sheets besides concrete boundary walls. The 
teams had to cross all these obstacles without getting noticed by the hiding terrorist. The teams kept on advancing towards the 
target location besides taking in hand the evacuation of civilians in the locality. The hiding terrorist suspected movement of 
forces, lobbed grenades towards the operational teams in which they had a harrow escape. 


Meanwhile, a small team led by SP Tanushree, IPS and DySP Mohan Lal alongwith their buddies after shifting 
civilians to next safer location was deployed in the backside of the target location which was an open field without any cover 
wherefrom the hiding terrorist could have managed their escape. However, the terrorist while noticing the movement of the 
party opened indiscriminate firing towards them. But both the officers kept on advancing thereby exposing themselves to heavy 
fire from the terrorist and forced the terrorist to push back. During the course, both the officers displayed indomitable courage 
to hold onto their positions thus exhibited exemplary and exceptional gallant of highest order. 


Another party headed by DySP Surinder Singh alongwith his team including ASI Narinder Singh kept crawling 
towards the terrorist. During the course, the Hiding terrorist was also offered to surrender to which he didn’t respond and 
instead hurled grenades in successions amid indiscriminate firing. 


Meanwhile, the Ist team led by SP Tanushree, IPS and DySP Mohan Lal aiongwith their buddies started advancing 
towards the hiding terrorist and in the exchange of heavy fire between the two sides involving the advancing parties and 
terrorist, the hiding hard-core terrorist was neutralized. Both the teams led by SP Tanushree, IPS and DySP Mohan Lal and 
DySP Surinder Singh retaliated the fire amid indomitable courage with utter disregard to personal safety in the line of fire from 
hiding terrorist, exhibiting nerves of steel and bravery of highest order eliminated the dreaded Pakistani terrorist in the face-to- 
face gunfight. Later on, dead body of killed terrorist was retrieved from the encounter site whose identification was ascertained 
as “Saifullah" R/o Pak affiliated with LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case 
FIR No. 95/202 U/S 307 IPG, 7/27 LA.Act, 3, l6 & l8 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Harwan for further investigation. 


The operation was concluded without any collateral damage and the elimination of said terrorist was a big jolt to the 
LeT terrorist network in South/ Central Kashmir Range. The killed terrorist namely Saifullah R/o Pakistan, A++ category 
terrorist of LeT outfit was also involved in a number of terror attacks. 


In this operation, S/Shri Mohan Lal, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Narinder Singh, Assistant Sub Inspector and 
Rouf Ahmad Mir, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher 
order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 9/l2/202].

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 03.0!.2022, a specific input was received from reliable sources regarding presence/hiding of some unknown 
terrorists possessing illegal arms/ammunition in Wanihama area of Zakoora Srinagar falling in the jurisdiction of P/S Zakoora, 
Srinagar. Swiftly acting on the tip-off and to track-down the hiding terrorists after proper identification, teams of Police 
Component Srinagar, Valley QAT CRPF, CRPF |]5th Bn and Army 24 RR were constituted to identify the exact location of 
the terrorists so that the appropriate action is taken without any collateral damage. The teams so-constituted while conducting 
intensified searches in the said area, succeeded in zeroing the target location where the terrorists were hiding. Suddenly,noticing 
the presence of forces in the area; the terrorists resorted to indiscriminate firing upon the forces with the intention to kill and 
manage their escape from the locality. 


Immediately, an operational plan was chalked out by SSP Rakesh Balwal, IPS, being In charge of the operational 
parties. As per the plan, the nafri was divided into different parties, one of the parties alongwith the adequate nafri of Valley 
QAT CRPF and Army 24 RR was assigned for outer cordon of the target area and another party consisting personnel of PC 
Srinagar, Srinagar Police alongwith sufficient nafri of Valley QAT CRPF/I5 Battalion CRPF was assigned to lay inner cordon 
of the target location. 


In addition to above, a special team comprising of SP Iftkhar Talib, DySP Mohan Lal, DySP Rohit Kumar, ASI Amit 
Raina, PSI Shivam Narang, HC Saleem Sadiq, HC Javaid Ahmad, HC Rinku Koul, SgCt. Bilal Ahmad, SgCt. Shabir Ahmad, 
SgCt. Abdul Rouf Wani, Const. Showkat Ahmad Dar, Const. Manzoor Ahmad, Const. Rameez Razaq, Const. Bilal Ahmad, 
Const. Mohd Rafiq, Const. Javaid Shafi and Const. Taveer Ahmed Dar alongwith adequate nafri of Valley QAT CRPF/CRPF 
5th was constituted. The special team of the officers/ officials was tasked to approach/advance the target location from all 
the sides for appropriate action and for final assault on the hiding terrorist, if required. The special team so constituted having 
taken all precautions while being in close coordination with other operational parties/ forces encircled the target location besides 
initiated the process of evacuation of inmates of adjoining houses to nearby safer place to ensure no collateral damage during 
the operation. 


As per the operational plan, the special team split into two groups; one group consisting of SP Iftkhar Talib, ASI Amit 
Raina, PSI Shivam Narang, HC Javid Ahmad Wagay, Const. Taveer Ahmed Dar and Const. Rameez Razaq & others while as 
another group consisting of DySP Mohan Lal, DySP Rohit Kumar, HC Saleem Sadiq Hajam, HC Rinku Koul, SgCt. Abdul 
Rouf Wani and Const. Showkat Ahmad Dar & others. 


In the first instance, the holed-up terrorist was offered to surrender which he didn’t respond and instead opened fire 
on the advancing special team with an attempt to break the cordon which was approaching the target. Upon which one of the 
advancing groups consisting of SP Iftkhar Talib alongwith his buddies positioned on front side of the target house and crawled 
nearer the target house wherefrom the terrorists resorted to fire on the operational party and retaliated the fire bravely without 
caring for their personal lives, displayed conspicuous courage while facing the terrorist and prompted the terrorist to run in 
another direction probably inflicting injuries to him during the course who, however, continued to engage the team in the 
gunfight. 


In the meantime, the 2nd group consisting of DySP Mohan Lal, DySP Rohit Kumar, HC Saleem Sadiq Hajam, HC 
Rinku Koul, SgCt Abdul Rouf Wani and Const. Showkat Ahmad Dar & others who had already taken position on the rear side 
of the target house which was an open area, retaliated the fire effectively and bravely thereby displaying conspicuous courage 
while facing the terrorist. During the course, both groups of the special team decided to eliminate the terrorist and for the 
purpose, intervention of the house was decided. Amid fierce exchange of fire, special team without caring for their lives 
advanced towards the target house amid indomitable courage with utter disregard to personal safety in the line of fire from the 
terrorist to hold onto their position, exhibiting nerves of steel & bravery of highest order and eliminated the terrorist in the face- 
to-face gunfight without any collateral damage. 


Later on, dead body of the killed terrorist was recovered and identified as Hafiz @ Hamza @ Abu Ukasha R/o Pak 
“A” Category of LeT terror outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 
02/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 3, |6 & 8 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Zakoora for further investigation. 


In this operation, S/Shri Iftkhar Talib, Superintendent of Police, Mohan Lal, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Abdul 
Rouf Wani, SgCT, Showkat Ahmad Dar, Constable and Taveer Ahmad Dar, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed 
conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023.

Dakhan Amroohi Teetwal area of P/S Karnah Kupwara J&K. The information without delay was shared with army authorities 
and plan was devised with Army 6 JAK RIFLES to launch joint operation under the supervision of SSP Kupwara.
