raw coverage, neutralized one terrorist at very close range with the assistance from other search parties who did not allow the 
terrorists to take any advantage till they neutralized one terrorist without caring for their lives, exhibiting utmost gallant action, 
killed another terrorist after a heavy fire fight. It was due to display of exemplary leadership, raw courage and true spirit of 
service of Inspr. Naseer Ahmad Malik, ASI Ghulam Mohd and SgCt. Reyaz Hussain Shah that one foreign terrorist was 
neutralized. Arms/ammunition and other items were recovered from the possession of the deceased terrorists. Case FIR No. 
64/2023 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA. Act, 3, l6, 20, 23 UA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Karnah for further investigation. 


In the entire operation, Inspr. Naseer Ahmad Malik, ASI Ghulam Mohd and SgCt. Reyaz Hussain Shah exhibited 
extreme professionalism coupled with raw courage and true spirit of service, who fought bravely without caring for their lives 
to reach to the target in extremely hostile conditions. Although the terrorist tried his best to cause damage to the forces by 
resorting to indiscriminate firing in which police party had a very narrow escape, however, they without losing their 
concentration and unmindful of their safety; fought bravely which led to neutralization of 0 dreaded terrorist. 


In this operation, S/Shri Naseer Ahmad Malik, Inspector, Ghulam Mohd, Assistant Sub Inspector and Reyaz Hussain 
Shah, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

terrorist in the house of SPO Abdul Latief at Saab Gali, upper Tuli Tehsil Chassana District Reasi. Accordingly, the input was 
analyzed/corroborated and immediately shared with Army 33 RR and a joint operation was launched. 


As per evolved strategy, the joint party of Reasi Police and Army 33 RR approached the suspected area by adopting 
the unconventional treacherous routes maintaining high level of surprise till gap free cordon get established. After laying & 
strengthening the cordon, the steps for search of terrorist in the suspected area was started by the joint teams of Police Reasi 
led by DySP Chhabil Singh, SDPO Mahore alongwith his parties with the assistance of counter part of Army 33 RR.

investigation. 


In the above operation, joint team of Police/Army displayed extra ordinary degree of professionalism, effective

In this operation, S/Shri Chhabil Singh, Deputy Superintendent of Police and Agya Singh, Head Constable of Jammu

On 04.0.2023, Kulgam Police generated a credible input regarding the presence of terrorists in village Kujar. 
Instantly, SSP Sahil Sarangal, IPS rushed towards the suspected village alongwith his QRTs & troops of Ist RR, CRPF 
l8th BN & D/24 Coy CRPF (Splinter) to cordon off the whole village and to ensure that terrorists may not get any chance to 
flee from the spot. All the entry/exit points, pathways and possible escaping routes were plugged off properly by laying 
concertina wire and physical deployments of SFs. 


As the village was cordoned off on war footing basis, SSP Sahil Sarangal gave special priority to save the lives of 
civilians by evacuating them from the cordoned area towards safer places as the suspicious location was highly populated. In 
the meanwhile, DIG Rayees Mohammad Bhat, IPS alongwith his QRTs reached on the spot to supervise the operation 
personally so as to conclude the operation without any collateral damage. After analyzing the situation, SOG/ Police 
Kulgam/troops of Ist RR/CRPF |8th Bn. & D/2l4 Coy CRPF (Splinter Coy) were alerted and put at strategic locations. In 
order to make the operation successful, two assault groups were constituted to neutralize the trapped terrorists. 


The Ist group headed by SSP Sahil Sarangal, IPS alongwith DySP Arif Hussain, PSI Rouf Ahmad Doom, SgCt. Nisar 
Ahmad, SgCt. Subzar Ahmad, SPO Hilal Ahmad, SPO Roof Ahmad, SPO Mohd Ramzan, SPO Shaheen Ahmad and 2nd group 
headed by DySP Abdul Ghafoor alongwith PSI Zahoor Ahmed Khan, HC Mohd Rafiq, SgCt. Ashiq Hussain, SgCt. Mir 
Nasrullah, SgCt. Nirmal Singh, Const. Javaid Ahmad, Const. Rafiq Ahmad, SPO Mohd Owais, SPO Ishfaq Ahmad, SPO Hilal 
Ahmad and SPO Mohd Rayees which were part of inner cordon; moved close to the target location & engaged in fierce combat 
with the hiding terrorists.

Despite heavy exchange of fire, the party remained stiff at their respective positions & showed professionalism,

to sacrifice their lives for the protection of national integrity and unity as well as to eradicate the menace of terrorism. The

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 5.06.2022, Shopian Police received specific information regarding presence of LeT terrorists in the house of one 
Hilal Ahmad Magray S/o Mohd Yousuf Magray at Kanjiullar Shopian and are planning to carryout terror strike in the area. 
Accordingly, the operation was meticulously planned by SSP Tanushree with Army 34 RR & CRPF 78॥ Bn. A team of 
Shopian Police under the supervision of SSP Shopian with the assistance of 34 RR, |78th Bn. CRPF, laid a cordon of whole 
hamlet of Kanjiullar, Shopian. 


The operational parties under their tactical acumen to reach to the target area without getting exposed. Two composite 
joint squads of Army/Police & CRPF were constituted, one headed by SSP Tanushree and another headed by Addl. SP Ifroz 
Ahmad for laying cordon of the suspected house. Both the parties started search and during search, the presence of terrorists 
was established and it has been ascertained that terrorist are hiding in a residential house and cordon around the target house 
was further tightened and started the searches. The terrorists sensing the presence of the operational personnel around the target 
house, fired heavily upon the operational men with their illegal arms/ammunition with intention to cause damage to them and 
to break the cordon which created a chaos among the civilians residing in cordoned area and around. However, the fire was 
cautiously & effectively retaliated in self-defense. 


The terrorists were offered to surrender which they refused and continuously fired upon the cordon party. It was 
decided to evacuate the civilians trapped in the cordoned area. The Joint teams headed by SSP Tanushree and Addl. SP Ifroz 
Ahmad including HC Reyaz Ahmad, Const. Mohmad Saleem Dar, Const. Mukesh Kumar, Const. Javaid Abass Mattoo, Const. 
Shakti Singh, Follower Ab. Rashid Dar, SPO Bashir Ahmad, SPO Waseem Ahmad volunteered to take the responsibility of 
evacuation of the civilian trapped in the cordoned area to the safer place. Despite the risk involved, they showed utter disregard 
to their personal safety, evacuated the civilians to safer places.

In order to eliminate the hiding terrorists without any collateral damage, assault teams headed by DySP Mohd Ashrif, 
DySP Imamsahib including HC Reyaz Ahmad, Const. Mohmad Saleem, Const. Mukesh Kumar, Const. Javaid Abass Mattoo, 
Const. Shakti Singh, Follower Ab. Rashid Dar, SPO Bashir Ahmad, SPO Waseem Ahmad, under the close supervision of SSP 
Tanushree volunteered themselves for the task to march towards holed-up terrorists in various directions thus tightened cordon 
further and swiftly retaliated the terrorist attack. As the terrorists were heavily armed, they started indiscriminate firing on the 
teams and lobbed several grenades thus making the task more difficult for assault teams to move forward. 


However, the assault team without caring for their lives showing nerve of steel, marched towards target in a fire 
retaliatory action and reached in close proximity of the hiding terrorists. The assault party headed by DySP Mohd Ashrif 
including HC Reyaz Ahmad, Const. Mohmad Saleem Dar, Const. Mukesh Kumar, Const. Javaid Abass Mattoo, Const. Shakti 
Singh, Follower Ab. Rashid Dar, SPO Bashir Ahmad, SPO Waseem Ahmad engaged the terrorists from one side. The holed- 
up terrorists sensing noose around their neck, came out from the hide (residential house) showering bullets. The assault teams 
led by Addl. SP Ifroz Ahmad retaliated the fire effectively without losing their concentration and eliminated both the terrorists 
in a face-to-face gunfight. The slain terrorists were later-on identified as Tufail Nazir Ganie S/o Nazir Ahmad Ganie R/o 
Ramnagri Shopian of LeT outfit and Jan Mohammad Lone S/o Ab Rashid Lone R/o Braripora Shopian of LeT. 
Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. |/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 
6, |9 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Shopian for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by Const. Mukesh Kumar, Const. Javaid Abass 
Mattoo and Follower Ab. Rashid Dar, who fought terrorists efficiently and intelligently put themselves in great risk; was 
remarkable as the endeavor to reach the target in extremely hostile situation was truly gallant. They showed courage in 
evacuating the people trapped in cordoned area 


In this operation, S/Shri Mukesh Kumar, Constable, Javaid Abass Mattoo, Constable and Ab. Rashid Dar, Follower 
of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Following a credible input on 02.0.2022, Police Shopian in assistance with 44 RR and |78th BN CRPF; launched a 
cordon and search operation in village Baskuchan. While establishing initial cordon, it was decided that civilians who were 
inside the village should be safely taken out from the inner cordon without getting panic or situation gets worsened. 


Accordingly, a team of Police Shopian in collaboration with 44 RR, |78th BN CRPF under the overall supervision of 
SSP Tanushree, IPS including DySP Raja Majid, Inspr. Atal Sharma, ASI Prithvi Raj, HC Gayoor-ul-Hag, HC Javed Ahmad, 
Const. Ghulam Mohammad Ganaie, Const. Rahul Kumar, Const. Abid Nazir, SPO Mohammad Shafi and SPO Ahsan ul Haq, 
were formed to start the search of target area. Police/SFs also ensured tactical deployment of all teams of Police/ SFs, besides 
placement of Bullet proof vehicles at all the vents and possible escape routes. 


As per plan, House No. 72 and its adjoining houses situated in the last part from North- West direction of village 
Baskuchan, were swiftly brought under the cordon. However, some suspicious houses were quickly cordoned off by the joint 
teams of DySP Mohd Ashrif including SI Mohd Rafi, HC Ab Gani, HC Khalil Ahmed, SgCt. Barkat Ali, SgCt. Elyas Khatana, 
Const. Javaid Ahmad & SPO Riyaz Ahmad alongwith his team 44 RR & 78th BN CRPF. After plugging off all the vents, the 
civilians trapped into the encounter site were tactfully & strategically evacuated with the team headed by DySP Mohd Ashrif. 
The search was initiated and during search notorious terrorist namely Naseer Ahmad Bhat R/o Nowpora Baskuchan got trapped 
in the house No.72. After tactical placement of SFs, the house owner of target house was questioned regarding presence of 
actual number of terrorists in his house and he confirmed that only one terrorist namely Naseer Ahmad Bhat is hiding in his 


74 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


house with AK like weapon. Further, some locals also informed that during pre-dawn, two unknown suspected persons 
equipped with Rifles were found roaming in the village but the whereabouts of the suspected persons is not confirmed. 


After tactical placement of assault teams at the firebases, the trapped terrorist was asked to surrender but he refused. 
After sometime when the movement of terrorist disappear, drones were flown to locate his position but could not locate. When 
one drone was sent near the bathroom side, the hiding terrorist was snapped in the drone feed at bathroom. As per the snap shot 
of the drone feed, the terrorist was positioned tactfully in the bathroom and there was no escaping route to flee. Sensing the 
situation, hiding terrorist started indiscriminate fire with targeting one by one upon the assault teams’ position in which team 
of DySP Mohd Ashrif decided to form the firebases around the target house and sealed the area. Accordingly, four houses 
adjoining to the target house were formed into the fire base with assault team of DySP Mohd had a very narrow escape as some 
of the bullets were directly hitting and piercing into the walls of House ०.7] but they managed to save themselves. After 
sometimes when firing was completely stopped, and searches with all precautions were conducted due to confirm possibility 
of another hiding terrorist in the area, the whole area/nearby areas were searched thoroughly but no other terrorist could be 
found in the cordon area. The dead body of one neutralized terrorist was recovered & later on identified as Naseer Ahmad Bhat 
S/o Mohd Yousuf Bhat R/o Nowpora Baskuchan District Shopian of HM terror outfit. Arms/ammunition was also recovered 
from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 45/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act has been 
registered in P/S Imamsahib for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by HC Khalil Ahmed, Ct Ghulam Mohammad 
Ganaie and Ct Abid Nazir, who fought terrorists efficiently and intelligently put themselves in great risk; was remarkable as 
the endeavor to reach the target in extremely hostile situation was truly gallant. They showed courage in evacuating the people 
trapped in cordoned area thus evading civil causalities by presence of mind and good policing. 


In this operation, S/Shri Khalil Ahmed, Head Constable, Ghulam Mohammad Ganaie, Constable and Abid Nazir, 
Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

reconnaissance of the specific area in Down Town where the terrorists had been hiding/taken shelter. As per the technical 
analysis regarding location of these terrorists, it was revealed that the terrorists had been changing their location and during the 
course they moved towards uptown area of Srinagar. This information was subsequently shared with the senior officers.

No. 09—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
Lig Shri Raiz Ahmed Deputy Superintendent of Police GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 05.0.2022, Shopian Police generated a technical input regarding the presence of terrorists of proscribed LeT outfit 
in village Moolu-Shopian. The input was confirmed with sister agencies viz 44 RR and 78॥ BN CRPF. After thorough 
deliberation, it was decided that a Police component, RR & CRPF QRTs will move to the target area and conduct search. 


The joint search parties under the command of DySP Raiz Ahmed reached the specific place as per the plan and a 
cluster of houses of said village were cordoned off and searches started. The joint troops casually enquired from some residents 
about the presence of terrorists. In the meantime, terrorist hiding in the nearby orchard of village heard conversation of the 
operational personnel jumped over fence of orchard and fired indiscriminately upon search parties in order to break the cordon 
and make his escape good. However, joint team led by DySP Raiz Ahmed immediately took position and retaliated the fire 
effectively thereby didn’t provide any chance to terrorist to flee from the site. 


Immediately, two joint search parties were constituted one under the command of DySP Raiz Ahmed and another 
DySP Raja Majid under close supervision of SSP Tanusree, IPS. Accordingly, cordon of cluster of houses of the said Mohalla 
was further tightened and search operation started during which suspicious movement were noticed by the joint search parties 
and the terrorist, in the notion that he has been cordoned off completely, fired indiscriminately towards the operational parties 
and again tried to break the cordon and escape from the cordon through an orchard. The fire opened by terrorists created a 
chaos among the resident civilians. DySP Raiz Ahmed assisted by ASI Riyaz Ahmad Sheroo, SgCt. Rouff Ahmad, SgCt. 
Mushtaq Ahmad, SgCt. Tahir Yousuf, Const. Parampreet Singh, Const. Mohd Yaseen, SPO Tariq Ahmad and SPO Rumysa 
Jan volunteered to take the responsibility of evacuating civil population to safer places to avoid any collateral damage. Despite 
the risk involved, they showed utter disregard to their personal safety and succeeded in evacuation of civilians. After completion 


PART [-.86९०८. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 77 


of evacuation process, terrorist was asked to surrender, which he refused and instead continued indiscriminate firing upon the 
operational parties. In order to make the operation successful and to eliminate the terrorist, the joint search parties fired towards 
the terrorist in retaliation. Meanwhile, DySP Raiz Ahmed assisted by ASI Riyaz Ahmad Sheroo, SgCt Rouff Ahmad, SgCt. 
Mushtaq Ahmad, SgCt. Tahir Yousuf, Const. Parampreet Singh, Const. Mohd Yaseen, SPO Tariq Ahmad and L/SPO Rumysa 
Jan also crossed the orchard and reached close to terrorist in the cover of trees by crawling. 


The terrorist noticed that the joint operational parties are covering him on elevated surface and moving meticulously 
towards him, he tried to change his position and made an effort to escape through a pathway, but the party headed by DySP 
Riaz Ahmed reached extremely nearer without caring their lives, moved meticulously and fired upon the terrorist and eliminated 
him. After the firing stopped, a search was carried out while keeping the cordon intact. During the search dead body of terrorist 
was recovered from the encounter site. The eliminated terrorist was later-on identified as Arif Rashid Wani S/o Abdul Rashid 
Wani R/o Shirmal Zainpora Shopian of LeT terror outfit. Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain 
terrorist. Case FIR No. 206/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Shopian for further 
investigation. 


In this operation, Shri Raiz Ahmed, Deputy Superintendent of Police of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed 
conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On ..2022 at about 0630 Hrs, Shopian Police received a specific intelligence input about the presence of some 
dreaded terrorists in village Kapran planning to carry out major terrorist activities in the area. The said village which has been 
very vulnerable from terrorism point of view due to its topography, dense orchards and support base to terrorists and conducting 
of operation in such an area was a daunting task for the Police/Security forces. On getting this credible input, Police Shopian 
launched a joint operation with District Police Kulgam in collaboration with 34 RR, CRPF |78th Bn. and while establishing 
initial cordon, it was decided that civilians who were inside the village should be safely taken out from the inner cordon without 
getting panic or situation gets worsened. 


With the same drill, team of Shopian/Kulgam Police in collaboration with 34 RR, CRPF |78th Bn. under the 
overallsupervision of SSP Tanushree alongwith SSP G.V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, DySP Raja Majid, SI Mohd Rafi, ASI 
Manzoor Hussain, HC Koshal Sharma, HC Hilal Ahmad, HC Ali Mohd Sofi, HC Mohd Abass, HC Nisar Ahmad, SgCt. Ayaz 
Ahmad, SgCt. Karnail Singh, SgCt. Ifthikar Ali, Const. Mukesh Sharma, Const. Mohd Rafiq, Const. Mohd Shafi, Const. Bilal 
Ahmad, Const. Fareed Ahmad, SPO Showkat Ahmad, SPO Aijaz Ahmad, SPO Showkat Mushtaq,SPO Mudasir Ahmad & 
SPO Imtiyaz Ahmad alongwith team of 34 RR & CRPF [780 Bn were formed to start the search of target area. Police/SFs 
also ensured tactical deployment of all teams of Police/SFs, besides placement of bullet proof vehicles at all the vents and 
possible escape routes. 


As soon as this information was shared with all Police/SFs, whole area was immediately strengthened by the joint 
team of Police/SFs and intensive search was initiated to find the hiding terrorist. As only Nasir Ahmad Mir saw the terrorist, 
SSP Kulgam took him with his team to search house २०. 43 and after complete search of the said house, Nasir Ahmad Mir 
again revealed that he left the terrorist between Madarsa and Mosque. Hence, the terrorist might be hiding into these two 
structures. The search of other structures / houses was about to complete and the security forces were about to move forward 
from there to search the next area, sensing the movement of joint team of Police/SFs, the hiding terrorist started indiscriminate 
firing upon the search party. Immediately, DySP Raja Majid and his team retaliated valiantly but terrorist was easily able to 
save himself because he was hiding inside the Mosque. Sensing surrounded from all around by the joint team of Police/SFs, 
the hiding terrorist started a vigorous attack all around by aiming the positions of SFs. All the teams which were deployed all 
around the Masjid started retaliation to pin down the terrorist. The hiding terrorist was stuck inside the Masjid and there was 


7i8 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


no escaping route on the street which leads into the cluster of houses. As soon as the hiding terrorist jumped into the street to 
escape, DySP Raja Majid and his assault team under the overall supervision of SSP Shopian, saw him jumping and took a burst 
shot with barrage of bullets and killed the terrorist in the street. Despite neutralization of this terrorist, due to possibility of 
another hiding terrorist in the area the whole area was searched thoroughly but no other terrorist was found in the cordon area. 
The eliminated terrorist was later on identified as Anis @ Kamran Bhai R/o Pakistan affiliated with JeM terror outfit. The 
terrorist was operating between Kulgam & Shopian districts with the help of local terrorists(OGWs. Arms/ammunition was 
recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 234/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I. A. Act, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act 
stands registered in P/S Shopian for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by HC Ali Mohd Sofi and HC Koshal Sharma 
who fought with terrorists efficiently and intelligently without caring for their lives; was remarkable as the endeavour to reach 
to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. They evacuated the civilian trapped in the cordoned area thus 
evading civil causalities by presence of mind and good policing. 


In this operation, S/Shri Ali Mohd Sofi, Head Constable and Koshal Sharma, Head Constable of Jammu & Kashmir 
Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

No. |l—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Jammu & Kashmir Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
l. Shri Nassar Ahmad Shah Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 5/l6.02.2023, based on a reliable information provided by own credible sources regarding presence of some 
terrorists in village Sudhpora-Taad area of PS Karnah, a joint operation was launched by District Police Kupwara under the 
supervision of DySP Adnan-ul-Haq with the help and assistance of Army 3/8 GR. 


After laying the cordon of the target areas, search for the terrorists in dense forests was started by the joint team of 
Police with the assistance of Army 3/8 GR. Since the terrorist(s) who were recently infiltrated in own side were hiding in the 
dense forests and it was highly challenging for the police and Army to locate them. Thus, a strategy was devised to search the 
target meticulously by making multiple search parties mix with Police and Army and approach towards the target area to locate 
the hiding terrorists in the jungle. 


One among the search parties was headed by Dy.SP Adnan-ul-Hagq consisting of Army personnel and Police officials 
including Const. Parvaiz Ahmad Dar and Const. Nassar Ahmad Shah. The said search party with the close liaison of synergy 
with other search parties have started to move towards the target area bit by bit and the terrorists soon sensed the movement of 
the search party and opened heavy and indiscriminate fire upon the search party which was retaliated effectively by with utmost 
bravery, highest class of professionalism with meticulous and learned drill. The search party under the command of DySP 
Adnan-ul-Haq, Const. Parvaiz Ahmad Dar and Const. Nassar Ahmad Shah have played a very pivotal role without caring for 
their valuable lives charged upon the hiding terrorists with the cooperation of other search parties who re-enforced the party 
from other sides till a highly trained and dreaded terrorists got neutralized from whose possession a large number of 
arms/ammunitions was recovered. 


The operation ended up with the great success without any collateral damage to own party. The killing of a dreaded 
and highly trained terrorist who had infiltrated recently into own area across the LoC to carry out the subversive activities in 
our territory, was a big jolt. During preliminary investigation, the killed terrorist was identified as Asif (FT) R/o Gaasla Leepa 
PoK affiliated with proscribed terror outfit LeT. The terrorist was on way to carry action against own force deployed on LoC 
and thus major tragedy was thwarted. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 
3/2023 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 L.A.Act, 3, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Karnah. 


In this operation, Shri Nassar Ahmad Shah, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 20.2.2022, Shopian Police generated a technical input through ESU Shopian regarding the presence of terrorists 
in the orchards of village Mujmarg Zainapora District Shopian. The input was followed meticulously and a joint operation in 
assistance with Ist RR and 78th BN CRPF was launched in the said village and search operation started. The hiding terrorists, 
on noticing the presence of troops, came out all of a sudden and started indiscriminate firing followed by grenade lobbing in 
order to break the cordon and make their escape good. However, DySP Raiz Ahmed, Inspr. Ghulam Hassan, Inspr. Atal Sharma, 
SgCt. Mohd Iqbal, Const. Shakti Singh, Const. Shanti Saroop, SPO Aakif Mohammad Wani, SPO Shahid Shafi, SPO Mohd 
Amin exhibited extra ordinary courage and retaliated in full volume which forced terrorists to retreat. The terrorists were asked 
to surrender, which they declined and instead continued firing on the deployed troops. 


In order to make the operation successful, two assault parties were constituted first one led by HC Gayour-ul-Haq 
alongwith HC Javid Ahmad, SgCt. Zahoor Ahmad, SgCt. Manzoor Ahmad Reshi, SgCt. Sajad Mohiuddin, Const. Arshid 
Ahmad Sheikh, Const. Ajit Kumar, Const. Reshav Kumar, & others with QRTs of RR & CRPF and other party led by DySP 
Raiz Ahmed alongwith Inspr. Ghulam Hassan, Inspr. Atal Sharma, SgCt Mohd Iqbal, Const. Shakti Singh, Const. Shanti 
Saroop, SPO Aakif Mohammad Wani, SPO Shahid Shafi, SPO Mohd Amin and QRTs of RR/ CRPF. 


Both the operational parties started advancing towards the target and it was exceptionally brave effort from them to 
advance despite bullets which were being fired by terrorists. The operational team led by HC Gayour-Ul-Haq firstly ensured 
evacuation of trapped civilians from the cordoned area to the safer places under the safety cover provided by the team led by 
DySP Raiz Ahmed to avoid any collateral damage. However, terrorists in utter frustration again came out of the hideout and 
fired indiscriminately on these operational parties with the intention to kill them and escape from the cordoned area but HC 
Gayour-ul-Haq and his team without caring for their lives retaliated the fire effectively and did not provide any chance to 
terrorists to break the cordon. During the fierce face to face gunfight, all three terrorists were neutralized. The eliminated 
terrorists were later on identified as Mohd Lateef Lone S/o Late Nisar Ahmad Lone R/o Check Kachdora Shopian of LeT outfit, 
Umar Nazir Bhat S/o Nazir Ahmad Bhat R/o Bon Dailgam District Anantnag of LeT outfit and Danish Hussain Kakroo S/o 
Mohd Hussain Kakroo R/o Kazihamam District Baramulla of LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession 
of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. [58/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Zainapora 
for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by SgCt. Manzoor Ahmad Reshi, SgCt. Sajad 
Mohiuddin, Const. Arshid Ahmad Sheikh, Const. Ajit Kumar and Const. Reshav Kumar, who fought with terrorists efficiently 
and intelligently without caring for their lives was remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile 
situation was truly Gallant. Although, the terrorists tried their best to cause damage to the forces by resorting to indiscriminate 
firing in which they had a narrow escape. However, they without losing their concentration and in good application of mind 
fought bravely and foiled their nefarious designs. 


In this operation, S/Shri Manzoor Ahmad Reshi, SgCT, Sajad Mohiuddin, SgCT, Reshav Kumar, Constable, Ajit 
Kumar, Constable and Arshid Ahmad Sheikh, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage 
and devotion to duty of a higher order.

ly Asif Rashid Bhat SgCT GM

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 2.09.2022 at about 050 hours, Shopian Police generated a technical input from ESU Shopian through HC 
Gayour-ul-Haq assisted by his team HC Javid Ahmad, Const. Liyakat Ahmad Khatana, SPO Anil Raina, SPO John Mohmad 
Ganie regarding the presence of terrorists in village Shirmal, Zainapora of District Shopian. 


As per input, terrorists were in process to carry out major terror activities in the area. The conducting of an operation 
in such an area of said village which had been very vulnerable from terrorism’s point of view due to its topography, dense 
orchards and support base to terrorists; was a daunting task for the Police/Security forces. On this, Police Shopian launched an 
operation in collaboration with 44 RR, |78th BN CRPF and while establishing initial cordon, it was decided that civilians who 
were inside the village should be safely taken out from the inner cordon without getting panic or situation gets worsened. 


With the same drill, team of Police Shopian in collaboration with 44 RR & l78th BN CRPF under the overall 
supervision of SSP Tanushree alongwith Addl. SP Ifroz Ahmad, SI Nazeer Ahmad, SHO P/S Zainpora and team of 44/RR & 
78th BN CRPF were formed to start the search of target area. Police/SFs also ensured tactical deployment of all teams of 
Police/Security forces, besides placement of Bullet proof vehicles at all the vents and possible escape routes. To avoid any 
untoward incident, targeted area was covered from different directions. Initially some houses and a bit area adjoining to the 
target area was brought under the cordon tactfully by initial joint cordon party under the overall supervision of SSP Shopian 
alongwith HC Gayour-ul-Hag, HC Javid Ahmad, Const. Liyakat Ahmad Khatana, SPO Anil Raina and SPO John Mohmad 
Ganie together with team of 44 RR and 78th BN CRPF were formed to start the search of target area. 


Afterwards Police teams headed by DySP Raiz Ahmad alongwith HC Ravi Kumar, HC Reyaz Ahmad, SgCt Asif 
Rashid Bhat were formed for final assault to eliminate the holed-up terrorists in the cordoned area. Before launching final 
assault, besieged terrorists were asked to surrender, which they declined and instead resorted to heavy firing and grenade 
lobbing towards the deployed forces. At this moment, one terrorist was noticed moving alongwith AK like weapon in the 
targeted area. Seeing his movement few probing shots were given by DySP Raiz Ahmad and his team which was retaliated 
fiercely by terrorists. With utmost control over firing, cordon teams kept on engaging the terrorists whenever their movement 
was noticed. But terrorists were manoeuvring their positions from one side to another side and tried to escape from the cordoned 
area. Their firing was proving that they have equipped with deadly weapons and ammunitions. This indiscriminate firing of 
terrorists was proving to be very fatal specially team of HC Gayour ul Haq which was nearest to firebase and came under heavy 
volume of terrorists firing. However, they did not lose their concentration and continued firing on terrorists without caring for 
their precious lives. 


After a prolonged gun battle when there was no firing from the target area, final search was ordered and still all 
precautions were made to avoid any last moment loss. Thorough search was done in which dead body of one killed terrorist 
alongwith his weapon was recovered who was later on identified as Aqib Hussain Paul S/o Mohammad Akram Paul R/o 
Drawani Imamsahib, Shopian of LeT outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR 
No. I3/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Zainapora for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by police party specially SgCt. Asif Rashid Bhat 
and Const. Liyakat Ahmad Khatana who fought with terrorists efficiently and intelligently without caring for their precious 
lives; was remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. They evacuated 
the civilian trapped in the cordoned area thus evading civil causalities by presence of mind and good policing. 


In this operation, S/Shri Asif Rashid Bhat, SgCT and Liyakat Ahmad Khatana, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police 
displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 27.09.2022, Kulgam Police generated a credible input regarding the presence of terrorists in village Awhatoo. 
Instantly, SSP G.V.Sundeep Chakravarthy, IPS rushed towards the suspected village alongwith his QRTs, 34 RR & troops of 
CRPF 8th Bn. to cordon off the whole village to ensure that the trapped terrorists will not get any chance to flee from the spot. 
All the entry/exit points, pathways and possible escaping routes were plugged off properly by placing concertina wire and 
physical deployments of SFs. 


The Ist team was headed by DIG Sujit Kumar, IPS alongwith SI Sukh Dev Singh, SgCt. Abid Ahmad, SgCt. Mohd 
Abass, Const. Rafiq Ahmad, SPO Bisma Jan, SPO Khurshid Ahmad, SPO Ravi Kumar and troops of 34 RR/ CRPF |8th Bn, 
whereas 2nd team which was from the back side of the target house; was headed by SSP G.V.Sundeep Chakravarthy along 
with DySP Gh. Mohd Bhat, ASI Manzoor Hussain Peer, ASI Manzoor Ahmad, HC Nisar Ahmad, SgCt. Tariq Ahmad, Const. 
Rakeeb Ahmad Malik, Const. Mohd Iqbal, SPO Bilal Hassan, SPO Aadil Bashir, troops of 34 RR and CRPF |8th Bn. 


The teams as per operational plan moved towards target location from all sides. The Ist team was from the front side 
of the target house & as they moved close to the target house the hiding terrorists fired indiscriminately upon them with the 
intention to kill them and make their escape good but Ist party was monitoring the movements of the terrorists very keenly and 
traced their movement within shortest possible time and got engaged with fierce combat with the terrorists. During on-going 
gun battle, the Ist party gunned down one terrorist on the spot. However, 2nd terrorist was continuously changing his position 
by lobbing hand gradates and continued heavy volume of firing towards security forces, meanwhile the said terrorist came out 
in open and tried to break the cordon to manage his escape form eh cordoned area. But the 2nd party which was cordoning the 
back side of encounter site showed presence of mind and alertness challenged the terrorist in open & gunned down him without 
any collateral damage. 


The coherence showed by the Police party especially DIG Sujit Kumar IPS, SSP G.V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, DySP 
Gh. Mohd Bhat, SI Sukh Dev Singh, ASI Manzoor Hussain Peer, HC Nisar Ahmad, SgCt. Mohd Abass, SgCt. Tariq Ahmad, 
Const. Rafiq Ahmad, Const. Rakeeb Ahmad Malik, Const. Mohd Iqbal, SPO Bilal Hassan, SPO Aadil Bashir, SPO Bisma Jan, 
SPO Khurshid Ahmad and SPO Ravi Kumar, have played an exemplary role in elimination of 02 dreaded terrorists without 
caring about their lives. Also, these officers/officials showed presence of mind and make efficient use of strategic tactics in 
neutralizing the terrorists on spot without any collateral damage. 


The eliminated terrorists were later-on identified as Mohammad Asif Wani @ Yawar S/o Dilawar Ahmad Wani R/o 
Takia Gopalpora D.H Pora Kulgam (Category “C” of JeM outfit) and Mohammad Shafi Ganaie S/o Jahangir Ahmad Ganaie 
R/o Ves Batapora (Category “C” of LeT outfit). Arms/ ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case 
FIR No. 05/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 3, 6, 8, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Kulgam for further 
Investigation. 


The officers/officials of District Police Kulgam, 34 RR & troops of CRPF | 8th Bn. and their respective teams ensured 
safety of lives and properties of civilians during the successful operation. The professional acumen of the officers resulted in a 
swift and clean operation. Such a swift and clean operation gave psychological blow to anti-national elements and their 
propaganda machinery and at the same time provided motivation to security forces engaged in fighting terrorism in the area. 
The exceptional command and control resulted in effective cordon and resultant successful operation. 


It is pertinent to mention here that the gallant act on the part of DIG Sujit Kumar, IPS and Const. Rakeeb Ahmad 
Malik was exemplary as they faced a volley of burst fire by terrorists, but miraculously escaped from the bullets and grenades 
lobbed on them. 


In this operation, S/Shri Sujit Kumar, IPS, Deputy Inspector General and Rakeeb Ahmad Malik, Constable of Jammu 
& Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

S/Shri 

ie Davinder Kumar Assistant Sub Inspector GM

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 30.05.2022, Awantipora Police received information through a credible source regarding the presence of terrorists 
in the house/bakery shop at Chak, Rajpora. The input was analyzed/corroborated and immediately shared with CD 42 RR and 
CO 30 Bn CRPF. Upon confirmation of input, the operation was planned and coordinated with 42 RR & 30 Bn CRPF for 
cordon and search. 


As per evolved strategy, the composite party of Police Awantipora, Army 42 RR and CRPF 30 Bn approached the 
target house in evening hours and by adopting the unconventional treacherous routes, maintained high level of surprise till gap 
free cordon get established. After laying and strengthening the cordon; the steps for search of terrorist in target house was 
started by joint team of Police Awantipora led by SSP Mohd Yousif alongwith his buddies with the assistance of Army 42 
RR/CRRF ]30 Bn. 


No sooner the cordon was being established, the terrorists hiding in the target house started indiscriminate firing upon 
search/cordon party and also hurled hand grenades upon them so as to break the cordon and get escaped from the cordoned 
area but they ran away from target house and forcibly managed their entry into the adjoining house. But due to presence of 
mind, search party who were searching in the target house; quickly took positions and retaliated in self- defense thus foiled the 
attempt of hiding terrorists to escape from the cordoned area. As such the presence & exact location of the terrorists get 
confirmed; the cordon was intensified and retaliation was firmly executed by the joint team. 


Further on the guidance of SSP Awantipora, 02 teams of Police one led by Shri Mohd Yousif, SSP Awantipora 
himself alongwith ASI Dawood Ahmad, HC Yashpaul Singh & others with party of counterparts of Army 42 RR & CRPF 30 
Bn at Western side. Another team led by ASI Davinder Kumar & others with party of Army 42 RR, CRPF [30 Bn on Northern 
side, were constituted to occupy the positions close to the target house. As the target house was located in the built up area and 
there was every apprehension of eruption of law & order problem and in order to thwart any such attempt, the deployment of 
Police Awantipora & CRPF 30 Bn under the command of Shri Javid Ahmad, DySP, PC Awantipora was strategically made 
at the points in its surroundings so as to sterile the target house/area from any kind of extrinsic interference which might 
otherwise could have disrupted to carry out the anti-terrorist operation successfully. 


SSP Awantipora acted as per the situation on ground, revisited the strategy and advised DySP Javid Ahmad to follow 
his team from two different directions for safe evacuation of civilians from the target house & from the houses falling in close 
proximity of the target house. This was sensitive and vital stage of the operation to evacuate the civilians who were trapped in 
the target house and the adjoining houses, as there was every apprehension of the attack from the besieged terrorists. The dare 
devil task was conducted by Shri Mohd Yousif, SSP Awantipora alongwith his buddies amid the shower of fire from the 
terrorists. 


The evacuation of civilians was made possible with the help of covering fire provided by Police component 
Awantipora & CRPF 30 Bn. After that, hiding terrorists were given an opportunity to lay their arms and surrender which they 
ignored and in turn, opened volley of fire upon the search/assault party to break the cordon and get escaped. The composite 
assault party of Police Awantipora, 42 RR/CRPF 30 Bn spearheaded by SSP Mohd Yousif alongwith his buddies took cover 
and fought bravely from the front without caring for their lives and neutralized both the terrorists. The siain terrorists were later 
on identified as Shahid Ah. Rather (JeM outfit) S/o Gh. Ahmad Rather R/o Lurow Jagir and Umar Yousuf Seh (LeT outfit) 
S/o M. Yousuf Seh R/o Tukroo, Keegam Shopian. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case 
FIR No. 08/2022 U/S 307, 452 IPG, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6, 8, 23, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Awantipora for 
futher investigation.

The slain terrorist Shahid Ahmad Rather was involved in case FIR No. |6/202 U/S 3 ULA(P) Act stands registered 
in P/S Tral on 2.03.202!. He was motivating local youth to join terrorist ranks. Another terrorist a newly recruited joined 
terrorist folds on 3.03.2022 from district Shopian and got killed in infancy period of his terrorism. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by the teams especially by ASI Davinder Kumar, 
ASI Dawood Ahmad and HC Yashpaul Singh who fought bravely without caring for their precious fives was remarkable as

but had played a conspicuous role of bravery, courage, rare, tactical acumen & exemplary jointmenship which led to successful 
neutralization of these terrorists without suffering any collateral damage. 


In this operation, S/Shri Davinder Kumar, Assistant Sub Inspector, Dawood Ahmad, Assistant Sub Inspector and 
Yashpaul Singh, Head Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty 
of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On .06.2022 at evening hours, Pulwama Police has generated specific information about presence of terrorists in 
village Drubgam Payeen of district Pulwama who on the directions of their handlers sitting across, were planning to carry out 
some terror action in the area. 


Accordingly, the information was shared with 44 RR, CRPF 83 Bn and after threadbare discussion; it was decided 
to launch an operation in the area to neutralize the terrorists. As per the decision, a Police party including Inspr. Zaffar Iqbal 
Dar, SgCt. Bilal Ahmad, Const. Barkat Ali Dar, Follower Ab. Latif Ganai & others under the close supervision of SSP Gh. 
Jeelani Wani, rushed to the spot and launched CASO of the suspected area along with contingents of 44 RR and |83 Bn CRPF 
and started searches for the terrorists hiding in the area. 


During search operation, as soon as the search party approached the suspected spot; the terrorists present there started 
indiscriminate firing upon them with the intention to kill them and to flee from the cordoned area. The troops immediately took 
cover and retaliated the terrorist fire with great courage and gunned down one heavily armed terrorist in the initial exchange of 
fire, whereas the other two terrorists turned back and took shelter in a bathroom, adjacent to a residential house. In order to 
avoid civilian causalities, dedicated Police team on the directions of SSP Pulwama have evacuated the people including women 
and children trapped in the cordoned area to safer places. 


After successful evacuation of civilians, the hiding terrorists were asked to lay down their arms before the forces but 
they denied and continued indiscriminate firing upon the troops and attempted to flee from the spot but the search party, in 
turn, showed maximum restraint due to darkness. In order to avoid damages to own troops in dark night, the forces withheld 
the retaliated fire power, however the cordon has been further strengthened and all possible escaping routes were plugged off 
tightly to foil any escaping bid. During the pre-dawn hours of the next day, the besieged terrorists open indiscriminate firing 
upon the troops and once again tried to flee from the cordoned area. The joint parties commanded by SSP Gh. Jeelani Wani 
and DySP Saqib Ghani immediately came into action and retaliated the terrorist fire with great courage and dedication from 
the front without caring for their precious lives and gunned down the other two heavily armed terrorists without any collateral 
damage. 


During the pro-long gunfight which continued for whole night, three dreaded terrorists of LeT were neutralized and 
arms/ ammunition recovered from the encounter site. The deceased terrorists were later on identified as (C-category of LeT 
outfit) namely Junaid Ahmad Sheergojri S/o Gh. Qadir Sheergojri R/o Gadoora Pulwama, Fazil Nazir Bhat S/o Nazir Ahmad 


724 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


Bhat R/o Drubgam Pulwama and Irfan Ahmad Malik S/o Gh Rasool Malik R/o Arabal Nikas Pulwama. Good quantity of arms/ 
ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. A case FIR No. 44/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/25, 7/27 I.A.Act, 
6, 8, 20, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Rajpora. As such neutralization of above-named hardcore terrorists is 
a huge setback to terrorist cadres especially LeT outfitand is a great achievement for security agencies and also have a sigh of

the said house started indiscriminate firing upon the joint search party with the aim to cause damage to the deployed forces and 
to make way for escaping which has resulted bullet injuries to two Army Jawans of 55 RR who were immediately shifted and 
hospitalized.

Rasheed & Ct. Umer Sikander and components of 55 RR and CRPF |82 and |83 has been asked to take position on rear side 
of target house in the inner cordon and were briefed to remain extra alert & monitor the movement of terrorists trapped in the 
target house and keep the senior officers informed after the developments/ happenings.

but the terrorists instead of accepting the offer, fired upon the troops positioned in a lane leading to inhabited area under the

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 23.04.2022, following a credible input regarding entering of terrorists from District Shopian into village Mirhama 
Kulgam, SSP G.V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, IPS rushed to the suspected spot alongwith the troops of 9 RR/CRPF |8th Bn. 
Initially, cordon was laid around the said village and door to door searches were carried out through joint search parties in order 
to pin point the location of hiding terrorists and their subsequent neutralization. As the joint searching parties approached 
towards the suspected house, the trapped terrorists fired indiscriminately and lobbed hand grenades upon the security forces 
with the intention to kill them so as to manage their escape from the cordon. The fire was effectively retaliated, which resulted 
into an encounter. 


During the encounter, the trapped terrorists were changing their positions continuously from one place to another so 
as to flee from the spot and it was very difficult to neutralize these terrorists under such circumstances. However, as per 
operational plan chalked out by SSP Kulgam, special priority was given to evacuate the civilians from the encounter site 
towards safer places. After ensuring safety and security of civilians SSP Kulgam constituted different teams along with 
respective QRTs of Police & SFs to neutralize the trapped terrorists without any collateral damage. The first team headed by 
SSP Kulgam along with HC Rayeesh Ahmad Reshi, SgCt. Ichpal Singh, SgCt. Mohd Shaid, SPO Mubashir Ahmad, SPO Ishfaq 
Ahmad, SPO Majid Hussain and troops of 09 RR/I8th Bn CRPF and 2nd team which was from the back side of the target 
house headed by Inspr. Ab. Ahad alongwith Sgct Liyaqat Ali, SgCt. Taroog Ahmad, SgCt. Masood Ahmed, SPO Sartaj Ahmad, 
SPO Parveez Ahmad, SPO Mushtaq Ahmad, SPO Rayees Ahmad and troops of 09 २२/| 80 Bn CRPF. 


The teams as per operational plan moved close towards the target house, but the terrorists lobbed hand grenades and 
started heavy volume of fire upon the operational teams and managed to flee from the spot. However, SSP Kulgam alongwith 
troops of 09 RR/CRPF 8th Bn. Chased these terrorists in open without caring about their personal safety and security. Besides, 
all escape routes were plugged off within shortest possible time and the operational teams under close supervision of SSP 
Kulgam traced them promptly and engaged in fierce combat with terrorists. During the gun battle, SSP Kulgam alongwith with 
operational teams gunned down these terrorists without any collateral damage. The act of bravery, professionalism and 
operational tactics shown by SSP Kulgam to neutralize these terrorists was highly commendable.

The coherence showed by the Police party especially SSP G.V. Sundeep Chakravarthy, IPS, HC Rayeesh Ahmad 
Reshi and SgCt. Pervinder Singh have played an exemplary role in elimination of 02 dreaded foreign terrorist without caring 
about safety of their personal lives. Besides, they showed presence of mind and made efficient use of strategic tactics in 
neutralizing the terrorist on spot without any collateral damage. Such clean operations are need of hour to eradicate the menace 
of terrorism and to ensure safety and security of peace-loving people of our country. The slain terrorists of A-Category JeM 
outfit were later on identified as Sultanat Pathan and Zabiullah, both R/Os of Pakistan. Arms/ammunition was also recovered 
from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 5/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 3, 6, 9, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded

heading towards a cowshed located in an apple orchard, the hiding terrorists opened fire indiscriminately upon the search party 
with their illegal automatic weapons which has resulted bullet injuries to an army Jawan of 44 RR namely NK Jatindra Singh 
who was immediately evacuated and hospitalized.

safer places and thereafter, the holed-up terrorists were given an opportunity to surrender, however they turned down the offer 
and continued firing upon the troops. Upon this, the Police/Army officers on spot alerted the troops and briefed them to plug 
all lanes, by-lanes and interior path ways strongly.

location through which a Nallah is passing. In the meantime, the besieged terrorists opened indiscriminate fire upon the troops 
and tried to escape through Orchard area, however the dedicated joint party headed by DySP Shukat Rafiq Wani, immediately 
came into action and gunned down one terrorist. Upon this, the 2nd terrorist returned and in state of confusion, first enters in 
the cowshed and then attempted to escape through Nallah, however, the 2nd joint party headed by ASI Nazir Ahmad promptly 
came into action and retaliated with dedication/courage and eliminated the said terrorist too. During searches two dead bodies 
of the terrorists were recovered and later- on identified as Aijaz Ahmad Hafiz S/o Faroog Ahmad Hafiz R/o Dalipora Pulwama 
and Shahid Ayoub Sheikh S/o Mohd Ayoub Sheikh R/o Deeri Murran of Al-Badr outfit. Arms/ammunition was recovered from 
the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. /2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6, 8, 9, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands 
registered in P/S Pulwama for further investigation. 


The killed hard-core terrorists were involved in various terror attacks including attacks on non—local workers, PRIs 
and other subversive activities; as such their elimination is a great success for Police/SFs and sigh of relief for peace loving 
citizens.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 23.08.202] at about 9:05 hours, based upon specific intelligence regarding presence of terrorists in village Peth- 
Seer, Sopore; a joint cordon and search operation was launched in the area by Sopore Police, Army 52-RR and [77 Bn. CRPF. 
Accordingly, as per planned strategy; a joint team of Police/ Army/ CRPF including DySP Farha Nishat, SgCt Mohd Younis 
Rather & others headed by Shri Mohd Ameen Bhat, was constituted as an advance team to evacuate the trapped civilians first 
under the backup/covering firing of 2nd joint party. 


Accordingly, DySP Mohd Ameen Bhat led his team and started approaching towards the target house under the backup 
fire of back up party. As soon as the advance team was about to reach the target house, the terrorists after sensing that the 
security forces are about to reach the target house, the terrorists three in number, suddenly jumped out from the house while 
showering volume of bullets and tried to escape from the spot but before the terrorists escape from the spot, the advance team 
especially DySP Farha Nishat, SgCt. Mohd Younis Rather remained determined like a rock and retaliated the fire effectively 
and in the ensuing face to face gunfight three dreaded terrorists belonging to proscribed Lashker-i- Toiba (LeT) terror outfit 
were neutralized. The identity of the slain terrorists was later on ascertained as Faisal Fayaz S/o Fayaz Ahmad Thokar R/o 
Kapran District Shopian, Rameez Ahmad Ganai S/o Abdul Majeed Ganai R/o Zipora-Dharamati District Shopian and Ghulam 
Mustafa Sheikh S/o Abdul Ahad Shiekh R/o Tikkipora Lolab District Kupwara. Arms/ammunition was recovered from the 
possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 99/202! U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act stands registered in P/S Tarzoo for further 
investigation. 


The area of operation was densely populated and the operation was concluded in evening hours and in such a volatile 
situation, it was a big challenge for the security forces to launch such a successful clean anti-terrorist operation, but it was 
conspicuous bravery, leadership qualities, professionalism and matchless resilience exhibited especially by DySP Farha Nishat, 
SgCt. Mohd Younis Rather, that the operation was concluded with the elimination of three local terrorists of Lashker-i-Toiba 
terror outfit without any collateral damage. While fighting with the terrorists, they put their lives at a great risk in the instant 
operation to uphold the security/ sovereignty and integrity of the India. 


In this operation, Smt. Farha Nishat, Deputy Superintendent of Police and Shri Mohd Younis Rather, SgCT of Jammu 
& Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 28.!2.2022 in the wee hours, movement of a suspicious truck bearing Registration No. JKI8-226 (without High 
Security Registration Plate) was noticed at SOG Check Point (Naka), Kunjwani which was signaled to stop but the driver 
accelerated and fled away from the spot on NHW towards Kashmir. Subsequently, the truck headed towards Kashmir and on 
the way, broke the barricades of Bravo-03 Naka falling under the jurisdiction of P/S Bagh-e- Bahu. However, Police party 
managed to stop the truck at Sidhra Bridge Check-point at 07l5 hours and in the meantime, SP PC Jammu also reached on 
spot. At the same instance, terrorists hiding in the specially designed cavity inside the truck opened volley of fire on the police 
party which was firstly retaliated by SP ops Jammu, he and his team had a narrow escape from being targeted, at the same time, 
one team of Ist Para led by Major Umesh Lamba also joined the SP Ops Jammu. On this, additional Police teams led by SSP 
Jammu and QRT have reached the spot and took positions and zeroed in the terrorists hiding in the truck. 


SSP Jammu and SP(Ops) Jammu immediately planned the operation on the spot and divided the operation teams into 
two striking teams. A party under the command of SSP Chanden Kohli cordoned the truck and kept on the sideways of the said 
vehicle and another party under the command of SP Naresh Singh assisted by DySP Sunil Singh Jasrotia, DySP Javed Ahmed, 
Inspr. Azad Ahmad, Inspr. Arjun Magotra, ASI Kashmir Singh were placed immediately on the backside of the said vehicle 
with other QRT’s of SOG also deployed at strategic and tactical locations. Both the teams with the assistance of team of Ist 
Para were located very close to the truck and were in the firing range of terrorists but despite this the officers/officials displayed 
utmost courage and bravery in holding their positions and retaliated the fire of the terrorists. Both the striking teams were fired 
upon by the hiding terrorists with automatic weapons, UBGL Grenades as the terrorists were desperate and tried to break the 
cordon to escape from the encounter site but both the striking party retaliated professionally and fought very bravely and 
tactfully. During the initial exchange of fire one terrorist was eliminated by the SOG team. 


The terrorists kept on repeatedly firing from inside the vehicle on the police party but the Police party did not let them 
escape and kept them contained and engaged inside the vehicle only. The terrorist immediately started firing with UBGL and 
also threw hand grenades on the vehicle of SP Ops Jammu Sh. Naresh Singh in which the officer had a narrow escape but one 
of his PSO Rampal got injured and he was immediately evacuated by the SOG personnel but heavy firing from the terrorists 
continued. Both the striking parties led by SP OPS Jammu and SSP Jammu retaliated with full force and in the exchange of 
fire by the striking parties, resulted in the elimination of one more terrorist but other two terrorists remained holed up inside 
the truck and kept on firing upon the Security Forces as they had made proper hideout inside the truck. 


ADGP Mukesh Singh, DIG Vivek Gupta were supervising the operation at the encounter site and coordinating/ leading 
the troops from the front for achieving the desired results without any collateral damage. ADGP Jammu gave sufficient 
opportunity to the remaining terrorists to surrender but the terrorists being hardcore and highly trained who had infiltrated into 
the Indian Territory with specific targets refused to surrender and kept on firing on the operational party. Subsequently, as the 
encounter progressed, the troops of lst Para SF and SOG teams led by SP(Ops) Jammu with tactical precision, neutralized the 
remaining two terrorists with precise retaliatory fire. The prompt and tactical maneuvers of the operational teams helped in 
elimination of all the four terrorists during the fierce gun fight which lasted for more than three hours. 


To ensure effective aerial surveillance of the encounter site and ensure elimination of terrorists, the drone of SOG 
Jammu was also flown over the target area. The operation ended with the elimination of 04 dreaded foreign terrorists without 
collateral damage and huge quantity of arms/ammunition were recovered. Soon after that the bodies of all four terrorists (All 
FTs and Unidentified affiliated with JeM Outfit) were recovered from encounter site along with huge cache of 
Arms/Ammunition/explosives and other incriminating material and taken into custody. A case FIR No. 435/2022 u/s 307/20- 
B/I2/22/I23 IPC, 7/25/26/27 Arms Act, |6/8 ULA(P) Act was registered at P/S Nagrota for further investigation. 


The whole operation was conducted without an iota of collateral damage only because of the outstanding command 
and control exercised by Ops. team conjugated with the display of the rarest of the rare bravery and extreme devotion to the 
duty by them. The members of the operational teams generally and especially SP Naresh Singh, DySP Sunil Singh Jasrotia, 
PSI Nitish Khajuria, ASI Subash Chander, ASI Kashmir Singh, SgCt. Sandeep Kumar and SgCt. Shabir Ahmed saved JK UT 
from extreme danger of terror attack and had displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage, bravery and devotion of the highest 
order and performed exceptionally in the operation to eliminate all 04 foreign terrorists (FTs) who were fidayeens (A++ 
category), highly radicalized, trained and were carrying war like stores with them with the motive to strike the high value 
targets and disrupt the upcoming VVIP visit in Jammu and subsequently committed to give big fight for longer duration. 


In this operation, S/Shri Naresh Singh, Superintendent of Police, Sunil Singh Jasrotia, Deputy Superintendent of 
Police, Nitish Khajuria, Police Sub Inspector, Subash Chander, Assistant Sub Inspector, Kashmir Singh, Assistant Sub

Inspector, Sandeep Kumar, SgCT and Shabir Ahmed, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded

Sector in the jurisdiction of Police Station Poonch. On generating this information, a party of SOG Poonch and Army launched

movement of 3/4 terrorists was spotted by ambush cum surveillance party. On being challenged by the operation party, terrorists 
opened indiscriminate fire with AK alongwith UBGL that was retaliated with precision fire. In the ensuing fire fight, one 
terrorist was neutralized and three terrorists sustained grievous injuries. One body of unidentified terrorist was recovered 
alongwith arms/ammunition. The whole operation was launched strategically under the overall supervision of Dr. Vinod 
Kumar, IPS SSP Poonch, CO 5 Rajput Durga Battalion and DySP Manish Kumar (PC) Poonch resulting neutralization one 
dreaded foreign terrorist on LoC in Degwar Sector. The operation continued till !300 hours on 30.08.202. Case FIR No. 
25/202] U /S 307 /I20-B/I2/l22 IPC 7/25/26/27 I.A.Act, 2/3 E&IMCO Act was registered at P/S Poonch for further 
investigation. 


It was the highest degree of professionalism and courage and synergic exercise that Army column and police 
succeeded in elimination of one dreaded terrorist and grievously injuring three other terrorists, besides the dead body of the 
slain terrorist was retrieved from the close proximity of LoC without any collateral damage. Later killed terrorist was identified 
as Mohd Akram @ Anzla Bhai @ Abzla Ghazi S/o Sardar R/o Gujran Wala Pak. The whole operation was conducted without 
collateral damage only because of outstanding command and control exercised by Dr. Vinod Kumar, SSP Poonch, conjugated 
with the display of the rarest of the rare bravery and extreme devotion to duty as well as exemplary bravery by other members 
of Police team generally and especially DySP Manish Kumar. 


In this operation, Shri Manish Kumar, Deputy Superintendent of Police of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed 
conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

730 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 25.2.202], a credible input was received by Police Awantipora from Police Kupwara regarding the presence of 
terrorists of proscribed outfit Ansar Gazwat-ul-Hind (AGuH) in village Hardumir Tral in the residential house who were 
hatching criminal conspiracy for some terror act in the area. The input was analyzed/corroborated and immediately shared with 
Army CO 42 RR. After meticulously chalking out a plan and taking into consideration all possible hostilities, a joint cordon 
was laid by Police Kupwara, Police Awantipora and Army 42 RR at village Hardumir Tral. The composite party of Army 42 
RR, Police Awantipora and Police Kupwara approached the target house by adopting the unconventional treacherous routes 
maintaing high level of surprise till gap free cordon get established. After laying & strengthening the cordon, the steps for 
search of terrorists in the target house was started by the joint team of Police Kupwara led by SSP Yougal Kumar & Police 
Awantipora led by SP Mohd Yousif alongwith ASI Parmeet Singh, HC Farooq Ahmad Awan, Ct. Safeer Lone, Follower 
Ghulam Jeelani Gojar (Deedad) & others with the assistance of Army 42 RR. 


On sensing the movement of forces, the terrorists hiding in the target house started indiscriminate firing upon them so 
as to break the cordon and get escaped from the cordoned area. But due to the presence of mind, search party who were 
searching in the target house, quickly took positions and retaliated in self-defense, thus foiled the attempt of hiding terrorists 
to escape from the cordoned area. As such the presence & exact location of the terrorist was confirmed, the cordon was 
intensified and retaliation was firmly executed by the joint team. Further, on the guidance of SSP Kupwara; 02 teams of Police 
one led by SSP Yougal Kumar along with Ct. Safeer Lone, Follower Ghulam Jeelani Gojar (Deedad) & party of counterparts 
of Army 42 RR at Western side, another led by SSP Mohammad Yousif alongwith HC Farooq Ahmad Awan and party of 
counterparts of Army 42 RR on Northern side were constituted to occupy the positions close to the target house. As the target 
house was located in the built-up area and there was every apprehension of eruption of law & order problem and in order to 
thwart any such attempt, the deployment of Police Awantipora and Police Kupwara was strategically made at the points in its 
surroundings so as to sterile the target house/area from any kind of extrinsic interference which might otherwise could have 
disrupted to carry out the anti-terrorist operation successfully. 


Since conducting the operation in broad day light is always full of challenges that too when reports of hiding of local 
terrorists is received. SSP Kupwara acted as per the situation on ground, revisited the strategy and advised SSP Mohammad 
Yousif to follow his team from two different directions for safe evacuation of civilians from the target house and from the 
houses falling in close proximity of the target house. This was a sensitive and vital stage of the operation to evacuate the 
civilians who were trapped in the target house and the adjoining houses, as there was every apprehension of attack from the 
besieged terrorists. The dare devil task was conducted by SSP Yougal Kumar alongwith HC Farooq Ahmad Awan, Ct. Safeer 
Lone, Follower Ghulam Jeelani Gojar (Deedad) amid the shower of fire from the terrorists. The evacuation of civilians was 
made possible with the help of covering fire provided by Police component Tral & Army 42 RR. After that, hiding terrorists 
were given an opportunity to lay their arms and surrender through the respectable of the area which they ignored and in turn 
fired indiscriminately upon the assault party. The composite assault party of Police Kupwara, Police Awantipora & 42 RR, 
spearheaded by SSP Yougal Kumar alongwith HC Farooq Ahmad Awan, Ct. Safeer Lone, Follower Ghulam Jeelani Gojar 
(Deedad) took cover and fought bravely from the front without caring for their lives and neutralized the said terrorists inside 
the house. The eliminated terrorists were later on identified as Nadeem Nazir Bhat (Category “B”) S/o Nazir Ahmad Bhat R/o 
Koil Shikargah Tral and Rahi Rasool Bhat @ Adil (Category “B”) S/o Gh. Rasool Bhat R/o Kanipora Dadasara Awantipora, 
both affiliated with proscribed terrorist outfit AGuH. Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain 
terrorists. Case FIR No. 55/202 U/S 307, 7/27 I.A.Act, 8, 9, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Tral. 


In this operation, S/Shri Yougal Kumar, Senior Superintendent of Police, Farooq Ahmad Awan, Head Constable, 
Safeer Lone, Constable and Ghulam Jeelani Gojar (Deedad), Follower of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous 
gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 25/l2/202].

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 25.2.202l at about 0300 hrs, Police Shopian received a specific intelligence input about presence of some 
terrorists in village Chowgam District Shopian. As per input, terrorists were in a process to carryout major terror activities in 
the area to threaten the general public. 


Following the input meticulously, Police Shopian launched a joint operation in the said village in assistance with 
Srinagar Police, 44 RR and 4th Bn. The team of Police Shopian/Srinagar in assistance with 44 RR & l4 Bn. CRPF headed by 
SSP Amritpal Singh alongwith SP Iftkhar Talib, DySP Raja Majid Batt, ASI Piyaray Lal, ASI Bashir Ahmed, HC Ravees 
Ahmad, HC Manoj Pandita, SgCt. Ishtiyaq Ahmad, SgCt Ayaz Ahmad, SgCt. Showkat Ahmad Ganai, Ct. Vicky Khajuria, Ct. 
Asif Ali, Ct. Yasir Hameed, Ct. Sartaj Hussain, SPO Nissar Ahmad, SPO Irshad Ahmad, SPO Ishfaq Ahmad, SPO Javaid 
Ahmad and SPO Balram Sharma alongwith team of 44 RR & l4th Bn. CRPF, were formed to start the search of target area. 
However, during spot interrogation of one occupant who found a bit nervous confirmed presence of two suspected terrorists in 
the residential house at Chowgam, Shopian. 


After getting confirmed about presence of terrorists in the targeted residential house, a bit area adjoining to the target 
area were brought under the cordon tactfully by the initial joint cordon team headed by SSP Shopian alongwith DySP PC 
Keller, ASI Prithvi Raj, ASI Bashir Ahmad, HC Javed Iqbal, HC Faiz Mohd, HC Ravees Ahmad, SgCt Zahoor Ahmad, SgCt 
Ayaz Ahmad, SgCt. Showkat Ahmad Ganai, Ct. Vicky Khajuria, Ct. Aamil Fasal, Ct. Mushtaq Ahmad, SPO Mudasir Ahmad 
and team of 44 RR/l4th Bn. CRPF, were formed to start the search of target area. The terrorists hiding in the residential house 
sensing the movement of operational parties opened indiscriminate fire upon them in order to break the cordon and make their 
escape good. 


The searching parties immediately took cover and retaliated with courage triggering a fierce gunfight. After that 
Police teams headed by SP Iftkhar Talib alongwith DySP Mohd Ashrif, SI Mohd Rafiq, HC Ayaz Ahmad, SgCt Chaman Lal, 
ASI Piyaray Lal, HC Manoj Pandita, Ct. Asif Ali, Ct Yasir Hameed, Ct. Sartaj Hussain, SPO Nissar Ahmad, SPO Irshad 
Ahmad, SPO Ishfaq Ahmad and SPO Balram Sharma, were formed for final assault to eliminate the holed-up terrorists. Before 
launching final assault, hiding terrorists were asked to surrender and despite repeated appeals, no response was given from 
terrorist’s side and they refused to surrender. Also, the families of hiding terrorists were asked to make emotional appeal to 
terrorists but there was no response from their side. When the cordon teams were being positioned to target the terrorists, they 
hurled a grenade on the cordon team, which was effectively retaliated. The hiding terrorists were shuffling their positions from 
one room to another room of the concrete house. At this moment, one terrorist was noticed moving alongwith AK like weapon 
in the targeted area. The hiding terrorists started indiscriminate firing from all around to break the cordon and make their escape 
good. 


This indiscriminate firing from terrorist’s side was proving very fatal especially for the aforesaid parties who came 
under heavy volume of their fire. However, these parties did not lose their concentration and continued retaliation without 
caring for their lives. Despite this deadly indiscriminate firing from terrorist’s side, SP Iftkhar Talib alongwith DySP Mohd 
Ashrif and his team bravely retaliated the terrorists’ fire from the front without caring for their lives. During minute search, 
bodies of both the eliminated terrorists were retrieved alongwith their weapons and ammunition, who were later-on identified 
as Raja Basit Yaqoob Najar S/o Mohammad Yaqoob Najar R/o Achan, Pulwama (Category “C” of LeT outfit) and Sajad 
Ahmad Chek S/o Bashir Ahmad Chek R/o Braripora, Shopian (Category “C” of LeT outfit). Arms/ ammunition was also 
recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 30I/202 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, 6, 8, 9 & 20 ULA(P) 
Act stands registered in P/S Shopian. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by SP Iftkhar Talib, HC Ravees Ahmad, HC 
Javed Iqbal and SgCt. Showkat Ahmad Ganai, who fought with terrorists efficiently and intelligently without caring for their 
precious lives; was remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. They 
evacuated the civilians trapped in the cordoned area thus evading civil causalities by presence of mind and good policing. 


In this operation, S/Shri Iftkhar Talib, Superintendent of Police, Ravees Ahmad, Head Constable, Javed Iqbal, Head 
Constable and Showkat Ahmad Ganai, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and 
devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 25/l2/202].
