Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 25.02.2022 at about l00 hrs, Police Shopian received a credible input about the presence of some terrorists in 
village Amshipora, Shopian who were in a process of carrying out major terror activities in the area to threaten the general 
public. 


On this, Shopian Police launched a joint operation with Kulgam Police in collaboration with 44 RR, l4th Bn. CRPF 
and while establishing initial cordon, it was decided that civilians who were inside the village should be safely taken out from 
the inner cordon without getting panic or situation gets worsened. A team of Shopian/Kulgam Police in collaboration with 44 
RR and |4 Bn. CRPF headed by SSP Amritpal Singh including DySP Raja Majid Batt, HC Zahoor Ahmad Wani, HC Mohd 
Shafi Dar & others, were formed to start the search of target area. However, all the occupants of the said village have initially 
denied the presence of terrorists. But during the spot interrogation of one occupant who was found a bit nervous, on little bit of 
coercing; he confirmed about the presence of two suspected terrorists in a cowshed belonging to Shakeel Ahmad Khan S/o Ab. 
Rahim Khan R/o Amshipora Shopian. 


Afterwards Police teams headed by SSP Kulgam were formed for final assault to eliminate the holed-up terrorists. 
Before launching final assault, besieged terrorists were asked to surrender which they declined and instead opened heavy fire 
and grenade towards security forces. At this moment, one terrorist was noticed moving alongwith AK like weapon in the 
targeted area. Seeing his movement, few probing shots were fired by DySP Mohd Ashrif and his team which was retaliated by 
the terrorists. During firing, the terrorists tried to flee from the targeted area, their escape movement was clearly visible and 
seeing movement of terrorists, SSP Kulgam and his team fired heavily on the terrorists in self-defence in which terrorists got 
injured and failed to flee away from the cowshed. 


The terrorists started indiscriminate firing from all around to break the cordon of Police/Security forces. During the 
fierce fire fight, two troops of 44th RR namely Sepoy Hanamant Dhareppa Ayat and Sepoy Lakashmi Narayana and one civilian 
namely Shakeel Ahmad Khan S/o Abdul Rahim Khan R/o Amshipora Shopian got injured in the said operation. All the injured 
persons were taken to hospital for medical treatment and later on, injured civilian succumbed to his injuries. However, the 
deployed forces did not lose their concentration retaliated the terrorist’s fire without caring for their lives. Despite this deadly 
indiscriminate firing from terrorist’s side, Shri Mohd Ashrif, DySP PC Imamsahib and his team bravely managed and retaliated 
to the terrorists fire from the front without caring for their lives. As the firing was very heavy, terrorists in their last fight stood 
up in desperation and as soon as they disclosed their position in front of cowshed, daredevil of SSP Kulgam and his team rained 
the bullets from three sides on the terrorists and in the firing two terrorists were neutralized. Search was done in which dead 
bodies of two killed terrorists alongwith their weapons were recovered and were later on identified as Muzamil Ahmad Mir @ 
Faizan S/o Abdul Hamid Mir R/o Chatripora Shopian (Category “C” of LeT outfit) and Sharik Ayoub Wagay S/o Mohammad 
Ayoub Wagay R/o Amshipora Shopian (Category “C” of LeT outfit). Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the 
possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 06/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6, 8 & 20 ULA (P) Act, 3/5 Exp. Subs. Act 
stands registered in P/S Heerpora for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by DySP Raja Majid Batt, HC Zahoor Ahmad 
Wani, HC Mohd Shafi Dar who fought with terrorists efficiently and intelligently without caring for their precious lives, was 
remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. However, they without 
losing their concentration and in good application of mind fought bravely and foiled their nefarious design. 


In this operation, S/Shri Raja Majid Batt, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Zahoor Ahmad Wani, Head Constable and 
Mohd Shafi Dar, Head Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty 
of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 25/02/2022.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On .03.2022 during evening hours, Police Pulwama has generated specific information about the presence of 
terrorists in village Chewa Kalan, Pulwama who were planning strategies to carry out terror actions in the area to cause damage 
to forces and to develop panic among general public. Accordingly, the information was shared with 50 RR and CRPF [82/83 
Bn and after threadbare discussion about the terrain of said village, it was decided to launch a joint cordon of the suspected 
area in the said village. After zeroing the suspected area strongly, the operational commander SSP Pulwama detailed a Police 
party including ASI Zahoor Ahmad Wani, SgCt. Shamim Ahmad Kooli, SgCt. Akthar Ali, Ct. Zulkarnain Ali, SPO Shahi John, 
SPO Javeed Ahmad Teli and SPO Shahid Manzoor Mir under the command of Addl. SP Tanweer Ahmad Dar alongwith QRT’s 
of 50 RR and |82/I83 Bns. CRPF for conducting searches in the target area. During search process, the joint Police/SF team 
has confirmed presence of terrorists in a local Darasgah (Darul-Uloom). 


Upon this, the deployed forces were briefed to be alert; however, they were briefed to take proper care of the religious 
Institution (Darasgah) while fighting with terrorists. While strengthening the cordon around the said Darasgah, terrorists hiding 
in it open indiscriminate firing upon troops with the intention to kill them and tried to escape from the spot as a result of which 
one civilian got injured who was immediately evacuated from the encounter site and hospitalized. Since the operation was 
launched during the night hours and there was every apprehension of human casualties, as such it was decided to evacuate the 
civilian people from the security holdup area to safer places. Thereafter, two dedicated joint teams of Police, Army & CRPF 
were constituted and deployed at very key locations to fight with terrorists dedicatedly and to neutralize them. 


One party headed by Addl. SP Tanweer Ahmad Dar assisted by ASI Zahoor Ahmad, SgCt. Shamim Ahmad Kooli, 
SgCt. Sajad Hussain Malik, SgCt. Bashir Ahmad, SgCt. Akhtar Ali, Ct. Zulkarnain Ali, SPO Shahi John, SPO Javeed Ahmad 
Teli, SPO Shahid Manzoor Mir and QRT’s of 50 RR & |82/l83 Bn CRPF; has been directed to take position in backside of 
the target location and the 2nd party headed by DySP Saqib Ghani has been deployed on front side (inner cordon) to fight the 
terrorists. 


After successful evacuation of the civilians and plugging off all the possible escaping routes and interior pathways, 
the hiding terrorists were asked to lay down their arms before the forces but they denied and opened indiscriminate firing upon 
the troops with the intention to kill them and tried to escape from the backside, however the joint party headed by Addl. SP 
Tanweer Ahmad Dar deployed in the area immediately came into action and retaliated the terrorist fire with dedication and 
gunned down one terrorist. Upon this, another terrorist turned back and tried to run away from front side of the target house, 
however, the 2nd joint party under the command of DySP Saqib Ghani taking position in the area fought bravely from the front 
and neutralized the said terrorist also. 


During searches two dead bodies of terrorists and some arms/ammunition has been recovered from the encounter site. 
The killed terrorists have been later on identified as @ Kamaal Bhai (FT, A-category) R/o Pakistan and Aqib Mushtaq Bhat @ 
Zulkarnain @ Toib (B-category) S/o Mushtaq Ahmad Bhat R/o Karimabad Pulwama, both JeM outfit. Good quantity of arms/ 
ammunition was recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 50/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 L.A.Act, 6, 8, 
9, 20 & 38 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Pulwama for further investigation. 


In this operation, S/Shri Zahoor Ahmad, Assistant Sub Inspector, Shamim Ahmad Kooli, SgCT, Sajad Hussain Malik, 
SgCT and Bashir Ahmad, SgCT of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty 
of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from /03/2022.

No. |27—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned

Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

l. Rayees Ahmad Ganie SgCT GM 

2, Ummar Majid Mir Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 02.02.2022, Police Shopian generated a specific input about the presence of terrorists in orchard area of Nadigam- 
Nowpora, Shopian. The village Nadigam which has been very vulnerable from terrorism point of view owing to its topography, 
dense orchards, support base to terrorists, as such, conducting of operation in the area was a daunting task for the Police/Security 
forces. The input was promptly shared with CO 34 RR and Commandant |78 Bn CRPF and after thorough deliberation; a joint 
CASO was launched in the target area to flush out the hiding terrorists. 


Accordingly, as per the discussed plan; joint teams of !78 Bn. CRPF, 34 RR and Police Shopian quickly laid the 
cordon under the overall command of Shri Amritpal Singh, IPS, SSP Shopian. As the orchard area was quite bigger and covered 
with heavy snowfall, it was decided to flow the drone in between orchard area of Nadigam-Nowpora village to find out the 
suspected hideout. During the drone monitoring, a suspected hideout was noticed in the orchard area. The hole of the hideout 
was open and an iron door was seen over the hideout. Immediately, all the joint teams were alerted and area was sealed to tight 
the inner cordon. Joint troops of Police Shopian including SgCt. Rayees Ahmad Ganie, Ct. Ummar Majid Mir & others, Army 
34 RR and |78th Bn. CRPF, were ordered to enter into orchard area from all directions and advance as per the plan discussed. 


As the joint forces were advancing towards the suspected hideout, terrorists sensing the movement of security forces 
resorted indiscriminate firing on the initial joint cordon party from good distance and run towards Nadigam adjoining village. 
At this juncture, an appeal was made to terrorists for surrender, but they refused and instead geared-up his speed with firing. 
As the orchard was congested with apple trees and covered with heavy snow, the joint cordon teams advanced tactically towards 
suspected hideout with speed. The fleeing terrorist kept on firing on the joint forces. Finding the distance is getting very close, 
the suspected terrorist heavily fired on the aforesaid joint team who were trying to approach him. At this time, the joint team 
came under heavy fire of suspected terrorist. Despite this attack, the joint team did not lose their concentration and continued 
to advance. This was the deadly attempt taken by the terrorist which showed that he was highly trained to operate the weapon 
during running position in snowy ground and adverse situation. As the terrorist was visual with sophisticated weapon and was 
firing on the joint operational party. In retaliation and using operational skills, joint party of Police Shopian, 34 RR & 78th 
Bn CRPF fired in self-defense. It was impossible to advance with iron shield as ground was covered with heavy snow. The 
joint operational team comprising of DySP Raja Majid Batt, DySP Mohd Ashrif, HC Ayaz Ahmad, SgCt. Shahbaz Ahmad, 
SgCt Rayees Ahmad Ganie, Ct. Mohd Yaseen, SPO Ishfaq Majeed & SPO Mohd Shafi rained the bullets together on the fleeing 
terrorist and neutralized him in their first thrust of retaliation. 


Though, there was no fire from either side after this exchange of fire but there were possibilities of one missing 
suspected terrorist in the cordoned area. Accordingly, a joint search of SFs was initiated to find the other terrorist hiding in 
cordon. An extensive search was carried out by the joint troops but despite combing the cordoned area, nothing was found. 
Then the body of neutralized terrorist was retrieved from encounter site and was identified as Umar Ishfaq Malik S/o Ab Ahad 
Malik R/o Bungam, Shopian. The slain terrorist was functioning as District Commander of Hizbul Mujahideen Terrorist outfit 
and was involved in various terror related incidents. With his elimination, the terrorist network within South Kashmir in general 
and in District Shopian in particular has suffered a major setback. Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession 
of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 0I/2022 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 I.A.Act, 6 & 20 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Imamsahib 
for further investigation. 


The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by SgCt. Rayees Ahmad Ganie and Ct. Ummar 
Majid Mir while fighting the terrorist without caring for their lives was remarkable as the endeavor to reach to the target in 
extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. Although, the terrorist tried his best to cause damage the forces by resorting to 
indiscriminate firing in which all they had a very narrow escape. However, they without losing their concentration, fought 
bravely and foiled his nefarious design. 


In this operation, S/Shri Rayees Ahmad Ganie, SgCT and Ummar Majid Mir, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police 
displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 02/02/2022.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 09.03.202l, Shri Mohammad Ameen Bhat, DySP, Sopore received specific information through human source 
regarding presence of terrorist Commander of Al-badr outfit in a residential house at Kanipora Tujjer. Accordingly, DySP 
Mohd Ameen Bhat alongwith his escort personnel did recce of the area to identify the target house and its adjacent area and 
the routes approaching the target house in a Civil Vehicle. After identification of target house, two QRTs of Police Component 
Sopore headed by Inspr. Zia-ur-Rehman and Inspr. Ishfaq Ahmad respectively, one QRT of PC Wadura headed by Insp. 
Parveen Kumar and one QRT of P/S Bomai headed by Insp. Imtiyaz Ahmad SHO P/S Bomai; immediately called to P/S Bomai 
where whole planning for execution of the operation was made and accordingly the suspected area and the target house was 
cordoned off immediately. 


As per the information of human source, the terrorist had to stay in the residential house only for half an hour. All the 
entry and exit routes were plugged off to ensure that the terrorist does not escape from the spot. During the process of cordon, 
the suspicious movement was observed in the already identified target house by the QRT headed by DySP (OPS) Sopore and 
accordingly the information was immediately passed to all other QRTs engaged in the operation. After sometime, the holed- 
up terrorist came out from the residential house and fired indiscriminately upon the QRT headed by DySP (OPS) Sopore and 
in the initial exchange of firing he got injured, however he despite being injured ran and managed his entry in to another 
complex in the vicinity. 


Since some |0-!2 houses were inside the cordon and the inmates of these houses were trapped in their respective 
houses and there were imminent chances of civil causality if the last and final assault is carried out on the very movement. So, 
in order to avert the civilian causality; the QRTs were put to task to evacuate the civilians first and in the endeavor; all the 
trapped civilians at least from l0-2 houses were evacuated to the safe places. After complete evacuation of trapped civilians, 
the cordon was more tightened by resealing all entry and exist routs, the holed-up terrorist was challenged to surrender but he 
instantly turned down the request and fired indiscriminately upon the searching parties which was retaliated immediately and 
effectively retaliated leading to a brief encounter. In the encounter one terrorist of Al-Badr outfit who was lateron identified as 
Chief Commander namely Abdul Gani Khawaja @ Skinder @ Qari Jaleel S/o Mohd Mukhtar Khawaja R/o Kralgund, 
Handwara got killed. Huge quantity of arms/ammunitions was recovered from the possession of slain terrorist. Case FIR No. 
/202 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act stands registered in P/S Bomai. The slain was a hardcore terrorist and since long had 
unleashed a reign of terror among the general people especially Political activists in the jurisdiction of Police District Sopore 
and Handwara by way of killing innocent civilians and was involved in a number of terror actions on security forces and Police. 


It was conspicuous bravery, professionalism and leadership qualities exhibited by DySP Mohd Ameen Bhat, Insp. 
Zia-ur-Rehman and Ct. Gh. Hassan Mir, in the instant operation and their contribution is praiseworthy and gallant by all means. 
This is because of their matchless contribution that one hardcore terrorist commander got eliminated without any collateral 
damage. 


In this operation, S/Shri Mohd Ameen Bhat, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Zia-Ur- Rehman, Inspector and Gh.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On the intervening night of 2!/22.04.2022, on the basis of credible input regarding presence of two/three Fidayeen 
Pak terrorists of Jaish-e-Mohammad (JeM) terrorist organization in Sunjwan, Jammu area; SP PC Jammu and SSP Jammu 
planned the operation and zeroed in the target area and planned to cordon the suspected Mohalla i.e., Green Valley, Gaziabad, 
Sunjwan and its adjoining area. Inputs suggested that these terrorists are specifically tasked to strike the high value targets and 
disrupt the upcoming VVIP visit in Jammu. 


After proper lying of the cordon, search operation was launched in the area to make sure that the civilians are safe 
without letting the terrorists to break the cordon and flee away. During search at about 0335 hrs, the terrorists doubted that they 
are trapped by the security forces and to show their presence with the intent to create terror by killing the security officials, 
they launched grenade attack on the BP Bunker of SOG Jammu and one Gypsy of police and fired indiscriminately on the 
troops deployed on the ground. During the heavy exchange of fire from both side, one officer of CISF got martyred on spot 
and HC Balraj Singh & SPO Sahil Kumar Sharma, got injured. Sensing the gravity of the situation, Inspr Nayat Ali assisted by 
PSI Mohamad Younis Khanday without caring of their own safety, braved the bullets and reached to the location of martyred 
ASI and evacuated his body. The injured jawans HC Balraj Singh and SPO Sahil Kumar Sharma who were also lying in the 
pool of blood at the encounter site, were evacuated after very hectic efforts by Inspr. Sudhir Sharma and Inspr. Jai Paul Sharma. 
The rescue team showed great courage and bravery in rescuing the injured jawans by braving all the bullets. The timely brave 
action by the rescuing officers was instrumental in saving the precious life of the injured jawans. 


Immediately after evacuation of injured, striking teams were formed. One was led by Naresh Singh, SP PC Jammu 
assisted by DySP Ops Javed Ahmed, DySP Ops Vaseem Mehmood, Inspr. Pawan Singh, Inspr. Arjun Magotra and other 
officials of SOG Jammu, 2nd striking Team was led by SSP Jammu Chandan Kohli assisted by SP Mamta Sharma, Inspr 
Sameer Jelani, ASI Balbir Singh, other officials of district police and also by officials of CRPF and 9 PARA special force. 


In retaliation to the firing of the terrorists, the striking teams counter fired at the terrorists and encounter started and 
firing continued for some time from both the sides. The terrorists were well trained and were carrying huge number of arms & 
ammunitions including IEDs and Grenades. One of the striking teams when fired on the terrorists from high terrain, they took 
the advantage of darkness and run towards an empty house and took shelter in it. The other striking teams cordoned that 
particular house from all sides to foil the plan of terrorists to cause maximum damage to the security forces. 


Both the striking teams were located very close to the house where the terrorists were hiding and were in the firing 
range of terrorists but despite this, the officers/officials displayed utmost courage and bravery in holding their positions and 
retaliated the fire of the terrorists. Both the striking teams were fired upon by the hiding terrorists very heavily with automatic 
weapons, UBGL Grenades as they were desperate and trying to break the cordon and escape from the encounter site but both 
the striking teams retaliated professionally and fight very bravely and tactically. 


ADGP Jammu, Mukesh Singh, IPS and DIG Jammu Kathua Samba Range, Vivek Gupta, IPS were supervising the 
operation at the encounter site and coordinating /leading the troops for achieving the desired results without any collateral 
damage and had given sufficient opportunity to terrorists to surrender and made repeated announcements but the terrorists were 
fidayeens, hardcore and highly trained Foreign Terrorists. By this time, it was clear that these terrorists were completely 
radicalized and have been tasked to carry out fidayeen attack. The striking teams kept on retaliated the fire of the terrorists 
during the night and hold their position till first light in the morning and ensured that terrorist could not break the cordon of the 
security force. 


Finally, the striking teams led by Naresh Singh, SP PC Jammu, Chandan Kohli IPS, SSP Jammu assisted by CRPF 
and 9 PARA carried out the operation to eliminate the terrorists. They were hiding inside the small bathroom which was 
separate from the main house. The terrorists kept on repeatedly firing from inside the bathroom on police party but the Police 
party did not let them escape and kept them contained and engaged inside the bathroom only. The terrorist immediately started 
firing with UBGL and also throw grenades on the vehicle of SP PC Jammu Naresh Singh and the officer had narrow escape. 
Some of the Armor-Piercing (AP) bullets also passed through the BP Gypsy which was just behind the vehicle of SP PC Jammu 
but both the striking parties’ one led by SP PC Jammu and second by SSP Jammu retaliated with full force and subsequently 
they were successful in eliminating two terrorists. 


The operation continued for seven hours and all the hiding terrorists were eliminated in the said operation. During 
search, two dead bodies of unidentified terrorists were recovered from the room alongwith huge quantity of Arms & 
Ammunitions. Accordingly, Case FIR No. 75/2022 UIS 302,307,332,[20-8, I2l- A, IPC, 7 27 l.A.Act, 6,l8 & 20 The 
Unlawful Activities (Prevention) Act, 957 stands registered in P/S Bahu Fort, Jammu for further investigation. 


The whole operation was conducted without an iota of collateral damage only because of the outstanding command 
and control exercised by operational teams conjugated with the display of the rarest of the rare bravery and extreme devotion 
to the duty by them. The members of the operational teams generally and especially Smt. Mamta Sharma, SP South Jammu,

Inspr. Arjun Magotra, SgCt Mahesh Chander and Constable Sukhdev Singh saved JK UT from extreme danger of terror attack 
as the eliminated terrorists were fidayeens (A++ category).

SgCT and Sukhdev Singh, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 0.I0.2023, Shopian Police received a specific intelligence input regarding the presence of terrorists of proscribed 
LeT outfit in village Alishipora under the jurisdiction of P/S Zainapora. As per input, terrorists were in process to carry out 
major terror activities in the area to threaten the general public. On this, Police Shopian launched an operation in collaboration 
with 44 RR & CRPF [780 Bn. under the overall supervision of SSP Tanushree, IPS including DySP Raja Majid Batt and his 
team consisting of HC Khurshid Ahmed, SgCt Shabaz Ahmad Ganai & others alongwith QRTs of 44 RR & CRPF [780॥ Bn 
and moved towards the target area and laid initial cordon. 


During the spot interrogation of a few occupants, one was found a bit nervous; who confirmed presence of two 
suspected terrorists in the residential house No.|2-B at Alishipora. The drone service was used to locate the exact positions of 
hidden terrorists and 02 hidden terrorists were seen at doorstep of house No.l2-B. After getting confirmation of hiding terrorists 
in the targeted house and to avoid any untoward incident, targeted house was covered from different directions. Initially, teams 
consisting of aforesaid police personnel and QRTs of 44 RR & CRPF |78th Bn. were formed to start the search of target area 
to ensure that terrorists could not escape. During search operation, target area was pinpointed/ zeroed by the searching teams. 


In the meantime, terrorists hiding in the house sensing the movement of operational parties opened indiscriminate fire 
upon the searching parties from House No. 2-B with intention to kill them. The search parties immediately took cover and 
retaliated with courage triggering a fierce gunfight between the terrorists and search parties. However, it was noticed that some 
civilians have trapped in the cordon area. It was decided to ensure their evacuation to safe place to prevent any collateral 
damage. The search party of DySP Raja Majid Batt took the responsibility of evacuation of trapped civilians and moved them 
to safer locations. 


Afterwards, Police teams headed by DySP Javeed Ahmad were formed for final assault to eliminate the holed-up 
terrorists. Before launching final assault, besieged terrorists were asked to surrender, which they declined and instead opened 
heavy volume of fire and grenade lobbing towards Police/security forces. 


At this moment, terrorists tried to sneak from target house towards the dense forest but they had no way to escape. 
The dejected terrorists find no option being surrounded by Police/SFs from all sides, decided to challenge the team. One of the 
terrorists haphazardly ran from target house while firing indiscriminately with AK series Rifle towards the South East. The 
terrorist was visual with sophisticated weapon indiscriminately firing on teams of Police/SFs. DySP Javeed Ahmad ordered his 
team to fire together and rained the bullets on the terrorist who was resultantly neutralized. Another terrorist got bullet injures 
and he hid himself and again tried to flee by crawling from cordon. But as the terrorist disclosed his position, he got trapped in 
the firing of Police/ SFs deployed in Darasgah (firebase) and the team lead by DySP Raja Majid Batt and his team alongwith 
QRTs of 44 RR & CRPF [78॥ Bn fired heavily upon the terrorist and neutralized him just ahead of 30-40 meters of target 
house. 


Afterwards, the joint teams carried out thorough search with due precautions to check any other hiding terrorist. 
However, only the bodies of two terrorists along with sophisticated weapons were recovered. The eliminated terrorists were 
later on identified as Marifat Maqbool Mugloo S/o Mohd Maqbool Mugloo R/o Chiegund Keegam, Shopian and Jazim Farooq 
@ Abrar S/o Faroog Ahmad Wani R/o Shirmal Zainapora Shopian (both “B” Category of LeT outfit). Arms/ammunition was

also recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 83/2023 U/S 307 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act and 6 UA(P) Act 
stands registered in P/S Zainapora for further investigation. 

The professionalism coupled with outstanding courage demonstrated by DySP Raja Majid Batt, HC Khurshid Ahmed 
and SgCt Shabaz Ahmad Ganai who fought with terrorists efficiently and intelligently without caring for their lives was 
remarkable as the endeavour to reach to the target in an extremely hostile situation was truly Gallant. Although, the terrorists 
tried their outmost to cause damage the deployed forces by resorting to indiscriminate firing in which they had a very narrow 
escape. However, they without losing their concentration and in good application of mind fought bravely. 

In this operation, S/Shri Raja Majid Batt, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Khurshid Ahmed, Head Constable and

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 9.07.2023, a credible information was received from own source regarding the presence of terrorists in village 
Khaki Patch Machil area of P/S Kupwara. The information without delay was shared with army authorities and plan was devised 
with Army 4 Dogra Regiment and BSF [40 Bn to launch joint operation under the supervision of SSP Kupwara. 


After devising a plan, suspected area was taken into cordon, divided the police and army/BSF in multiple columns 
and the parties so made positioned themselves accordingly and slowly started zeroing towards the targeted area. As the party 
led by Addl. SP Farhat Jeelani assisted by DySP Sarfaraz Bashir Ganai, and HC Fayaz Ahmad was approaching towards the 
suspected target, it came under heavy volume of fire from terrorists hiding behind the bushes in the forests. While holding the 
position, the column led by Addl.SP Farhat Jeelani assisted by DySP Sarfaraz Bashir Ganai, and HC Fayaz Ahmad charged 
towards hiding terrorists, displaying raw coverage, neutralized one terrorist at very close range with the assistance from other 
search parties who did not allow the terrorists to take any advantage till the said party neutralized the terrorist. 


Meanwhile, the other party led by DySP Ajaz Ahmad, DySP PC-Kupwara assisted by Const. Basharat Ahmad Ganai 
and Const. Shabir Ahmad Dar succeeded in locating another terrorist and without caring for their lives, exhibiting utmost 
gallant action, killed the 2nd terrorist after a heavy fire fight. It was due to display of exemplary leadership, raw courage and 
true spirit of service of the above-mentioned Police officers and officials that led to the elimination of two unidentified foreign 
terrorists. Arms/ ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. Case FIR No. 78/2023 U/S 307 IPC, 
7/27 LA.Act, 3, 6 & 23 ULA(P) Act stands registered in P/S Kupwara for further investigation. 


In the entire operation DySP Sarfaraz Bashir Ganai, HC Fayaz Ahmad and Const. Basharat Ahmad Ganai exhibited 
extreme professionalism coupled with raw courage and true spirit of service, who fought bravely without caring for their lives 
to reach to the target in extremely hostile conditions. Although the terrorists tried their best to cause damage to the forces by 
resorting to indiscriminate firing in which they had a very narrow escape, however, they without losing their concentration and 
unmindful of their safety fought bravely which led to neutralization of these 02 dreaded foreign terrorists. 


In this operation, S/Shri Sarfaraz Bashir Ganai, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Fayaz Ahmad, Head Constable and 
Basharat Ahmad Ganie, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty 
of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


During the intervening night of 27/28.02.2023, Police Awantipora received specific information regarding the 
presence/movement of terrorists in village Padgampora, Awantipora who were conspiring to commit terror act in the area. 
Upon confirmation of input, the operation was planned and coordinated with 55 RR & 30 Bn CRPF for cordon and search. 
The information was also shared with DIG SKR Anantnag. 


As per evolved strategy, the composite party of Police Awantipora, Army 55 RR and CRPF 30 Bn approached the 
target area by adopting the unconventional treacherous routes maintaining high level of surprise till gap free cordon get 
established. After laying & strengthening the cordon, the steps for search of terrorists in the target area was started by the joint 
team of Police Awantipora led by SSP Ajaz Ahmed alongwith DySP Hilal Khaliq Bhat, HC Javid Ahmad Shah, Ct Rayaz 
Ahmad Wani & others wisth Army 55 RR & CRPF 30 Bn. 


When one of the search parties entered Jamia Masjid, they came under heavy volume of fire from the first floor of 
Jamia Masjid. The fire was immediately retaliated by the search party. In the heavy volume of exchange of fire, one terrorist 
got hit and fell down whereas the other managed to escape to one of the nearby residential houses. 


During ensuing gun battle, two army Jawans of 55 RR sustained grievous bullet injuries, who were immediately 
shifted to 92 Base Hospital Srinagar. Among them, one Army Jawan Naik Pawan Kumar succumbed to his injuries and attained 
martyrdom whereas other Army Jawan namely Heam Raj was under treatment and was later stable. 


In view of darkness and chilling cold, it was very tough for security forces to ensure safety of every one and the 
evacuation of injured soldiers was a great challenge amidst the on-going firing. Police, Army 55 RR and CRPF 30 Bn carried 
out thorough search of the Jamia Masjid in which dead body of one terrorist namely Agqib Mushtaq Ahmad Bhat 
R/o Malangpora Pulwama was recovered. He was “A” category terrorist of HM/TRF outfit. 


Since the terrorists were hiding in the Jamia Masjid, there was every apprehension of eruption of law & order problem 
and in order to thwart any such attempt, the deployment of Police Awantipora alongwith major chunk of CRPF 30 Bn under 
the command of DySP Mumtaz Ali and counterpart of CRPF was strategically made at the Padgampora-Malangpora axis near 
the encounter site, whereas Inspr. Younis Khan alongwith CRPF contingent was placed in law & order mode on the rear side 
of the mosque/ Mohalla so as to sterile the target area from any kind of extrinsic interference which otherwise could have 
disrupted to carry out the anti- terrorist operation successfully. 


The other terrorist, however, took shelter in a nearby residential house in the garb of darkness and earlier cover fire 
provided by killed terrorist. The dare devil task was conducted by joint team of Police Awantipora, Army 55 RR and CRPF 
30th Bn spearheaded by DIG SKR Anantnag amid the shower of fire from the terrorist. The evacuation of civilians was 
ensured from the encounter side by party of Police component Awantipora led by DySP Hilal Khaliq Bhat with the help of 
cover fire provided by Police component Awantipora & CRPF 30th Bn. After a brief lull, a quad-copter was pressed into 
service by Army 55 RR to know condition of the terrorist. Images captured by the quad-copter suggested that the terrorist was 
presumably neutralized. This exchange of firing with hold up terrorist continued till afternoon on 28.02.2023. The neutralized 
terrorist was identified as Aijaz Ahmad Bhat S/o Gh. Mohammad Bhat R/o Teli Mohalla Syedabad Aripal Tral. He was 
affiliated with JeM terrorist outfit (Category 7 (7). Arms/ammunition was also recovered from the possession of slain terrorists. 
Case FIR No. 28/2023 U/S 307, 302, 326 IPC, 7/27 LA.Act, l3, 6, 8, 9, 23, 38 & 39 ULA(P) Act stands registered in 
P/S Awantipora for further investigation. 


In the entire operation, DySP Hilal Khaliq Bhat, HC Javid Ahmad Shah and Ct Rayaz Ahmad Wani were oblivious 
to imminent danger but had played a conspicuous role of bravery, courage, rare tactical acumen and exemplary joint 
membership, unparalleled courage and selfless devotion to duty which led to the successful neutralization of two dreaded 
terrorists. 


In this operation, S/Shri Hilal Khaliq Bhat, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Javid Ahmad Shah, Head Constable and 
Rayaz Ahmad Wani, Constable of Jammu & Kashmir Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of 
a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


JJMP is a banned extremist group which is active in many districts of Jharkhand state. They are notorious for creating 
atmosphere of terror and causing hindrance in developmental works specifically in remote areas. On receiving the input about 
presence of armed cadre of JJMP in forest area of Jobe, Katia, and Ambatikar under PS Chhipadohar, an ops “Jobe” was 
planned consisting three teams which were assigned three different target areas. Team-| under command of SI Vinay Kumar, 
OC Chhipadohar PS was assigned the Haratu-Jobe axis. The area in general, is hilly and covered with thick forest which 
provides very good cover from fire and this places extremist strategically to counter any action against them besides its 
extremely difficult for the security forces to operate in that area due to absence of proper road network that negates the easy 
availability of reinforcement in case of any emergency. 


On 04.06.208, team left for the target area “Jobe” which is surrounded by hill features from all sides. While searching, 
when the Police team reached near the target area from the Western hilly forest side they were fired upon heavily by the 
extremist group. Extremists, being on dominating position approx 40-45 in number, resorted to indiscriminate fire on advancing 
force personnel. Troops indomitable under vibrant leadership of SI Vinay Kumar carried out counter attack and neutralized one 
dreaded hard core JIMP Cadre namely Girvar Parahiya, S/o Chullrai Parahiya, Vil: Teorahi, PS: Manika. And Regular Rifle 
with magazine 0l!, Ammunition-270, Misfire round-l4 TVS Bike-02, Mobile-05, Torch-0l were recovered. A small team 
which was detailed to monitor the body of extremist, was again fired upon by the extremists from the nearby hill feature, with 
the aim to take over the body and weapon. Since this team was in open and had no natural cover from fire and were in grave 
danger, SI Vinay Kumar exhibiting high level of professional acumen, leadership, dauntless courage and bold fighting spirit, 
led his men from front. He along with HC Pramod Kumar Rai, CT Ved Prakash Mahto, CT Ramchandra Kumar Mahto & CT 
Krishna Murmu formed a small team and moved in the line of fire fearlessly showing utter disregard to their personal safety 
and retaliated the naxal attack with vigour and compelled them to flee. 


During the encounter HC Pramod Kumar Rai, CT Ved Prakash Mahto, CT Ramchandra Kumar Mahto & CT Krishna 
Miitmu followed the orders of his commander without any hesitation and fought with the extremists with extreme courage and 
vigor without caring for their life and kept moving in the direction of fire with great determination and dedication showing 
utter disregard to his personal-safety. 


This highest Jevei of gallant action, fighting spirit and fearless dedication to duty of SI Vinay Kumar HC Pramod 
Kumar Rai, CT Ved Prakash Mahto , CT Ramchandra Kumar Mahto & CT Krishna Murmu not only resulted into killing of 
one extremist but also, saved the men and material from any casualty or loss from the JJMP extremist group. 


In this operation, S/Shri Vinay Kumar, Sub Inspector, Pramod Kumar Rai, Head Constable, Ved Prakash Mahto, 
Constable, Ramchandra Kumar Mahto, Constable and Krishna Murmu, Constable of Jharkhand Police displayed conspicuous 
gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 04/06/208.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 03/05/207, personnel of the Ishvar Gota Party (C-60 commandos); personnel of AOP Kothi and CRPF personnel; 
had an encounter with the Naxals in Koparshi jungle. 3 personnel of the above-mentioned party were injured in the encounter 
and needed to be brought back from Kothi to Bhamragarh for medical treatment immediately. API Netaji Sukhdev Bandgar 
was ordered by the Superior officers to take 2 MPVs alongwith PSI Bhandvalkar and personnel of the Manohar Mahaka G-60 
Party and do the needful. The aforementioned 3 injured personnel had been airlifted hence API Netaji Sukhdev Bandgar 
collected members of the Ishwar Gota party and he alongwith PSI Bhandvalkar and members of the Mahaka C-60 Party, started 
back from Kothi to Bhamragarh in the 2 MPVS. 2 KM before Karampalli on their return journey, the 2nd MPV was blown up 
by a powerful landmine planted by the Naxals. The severity of the blast can be ascertained from the fact that the MPV was 
thrown into the air and landed topsy turvy in the adjoining nallah. The blast trapped tire occupants of the MPV and grevioulsy 
Injured APSI Manohar Kotla Mahaka, PC Suresh Telamai, HC Manohar Lachma Pendam, PC Prakash Ishwar Kannake, PC 
Atul Satyanarayan Yegolpawar and PC Hidayat Saddulla Khan. The Naxaiites lying in ambush immediately opened 
indiscriminate and heavy gunfire on both the MPVs. API Netaji Sukhdev Bandgar, PSI Darade and personnel of Ishwar Gota 
Party alighted from the lst MPV, took defensive positions and ordered the Naxals to stop firing, lay down their arms and to 
surrender to the police but to no avail. The Naxals were calling out to one another by names and were giving orders and 
instigating one another to kill the police and and snatch their arms and ammunitions. From this, It became clear that they were 
members of the banned CPI (Maoist) organization namely Bhamragarh LOS commander Dinesh, member Shilpa. Jivan, 
Praphakar, Permill LOS of Sainath, Nagesh, Madhuri, Jaishila and other25-30 Naxalites. 


API Netaji Sukhdev Bandgar, PSI Darade and member of the Ishwar Gofa Party then resorted to precise and controlled 
gunfire and ensured the safety of not only members of their MPV but also the safety and safe exit of the personnel who injured 
and trapped in the MPV which was blown up by the landmine. The combination of precise' and controlled gunfire and, effective 
use of under barrel grenades, broke the resolve of the Naxals and forced them to flee into the dense forest. The said encounter 
lasted for 35-40 minutes. In the subsequent combing operation, the police party could not find any articles, arms and 
ammunitions belonging to the Naxals. 


It is pertinent to mentioned that this this was a deadly example of the dastardly Naxal strategy of blowing up of 
vehicles transporting personnel of the security forces. This strategy accords the Naxals with a dual advantage. Firstly, they have 
ample time to choose a spot, make preparations and lie in ambush and secondly an Injured and disoriented police party is a 
sitting duck as a target. But API Netaji Sukhdev Bandgar, APSI Manohar Kotla Mahaka, PC Suresh Telamai, HC Manohar 
Lachma Pendam, PC Prakash Ishwar Kannake, PC Atul Satyanarayan themselves from the indiscriminate and heavy naxal 
gunfire but also gave a befitting reply thereby quelling the perfectly planned and executed Naxal machination. 


In this operation, S/Shri Netaji Sukhdev Bandgar, Assistant Police Inspector, Manohar Kotla Mahaka, Assistant Police 
Sub Inspector, Manohar Lachma Pendam, Head Constable, Prakash Ishwar Kannake, Police Constable, Atul Satyanarayan 
Yegolpawar, Police Constable, Hidayat Saddulla Khan, Police Constable and Late Suresh Lingaji Telami, Police Constable of 
Maharashtra Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 03/05/207.

No. 35—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Odisha Police:—

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 03.02.2024, SP Kandhamal received a credible information regarding the movement of armed cadres of CPI 
(Maoist) in Pokharibandh RF near village Kakerkuppa (Kamerkia) under PS Baliguda. Since there is a Maoist practice to 
ambush security forces, an advance party comprising of SI Amaresh Chandra Narendra and six DVF commandos; was launched 
to conduct recce. After getting dropped, the commandos set on foot towards the target area. At about 07.40 PM, l00 meters 
from the South end of village Kakerkuppa (Kamerkia), Naxals began unprovoked heavy firing from their automatic weapons. 
The police team urged Naxals to surrender but without any success. Ct Jeetendra Kumar Nayak received a grievous gunshot 
injury on his left arm and to defend himself began firing from his right hand. SI Amaresh Chandra Narendra divided the party 
in two flanks to save Ct Jeetendra Kumar Nayak. SI Amaresh Chandra Narendra along with OAPF Youdhisthira Mallick in 
left flank retaliated heavily upon the enemies and advanced while OAPF Arjuna Mallick gave support fire from the right flank. 
The courageous commandos overpowered the Maoists in a daring close quarter battle. Despite gunshot injury, their bravery 
forced the numerically superior and tactically better placed Naxals to retreat. The successful operation led to the neutralization 
of one DVCM/DCM cadre Dasru@Lakma Mandavi of KKBN division of CPI (Maoist) on whom the Government of Odisha 
had declared a reward of rupees five lakhs. 


In this operation, S/Shri Amaresh Chandra Narendra, Sub Inspector, Jeetendra Kumar Nayak, Constable, Arjuna 
Mallick, OAPF and Youdhisthira Mallick, OAPF of Odisha Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to 
duty of a higher order.

No. 36—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Odisha Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

ie Aswini Kumar Bagh Havildar GM 

2: Dambarudhar Bhoi Commando GM

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 08/05/2024, based on credible intelligence; SP Nabarangpur launched a search operation in the Hatigaon forest 
area under Raighar police station of Nabarangpur district led by SI (Armed) Partha Sarathi Parida from SOG AT No-08. The 
operation involved 46 police personnel, including 22 SOG commandos from AT No-2]. The team arrived at Hatigaon RF 
around 5:!5 AM on 09/05/2024, positioning them 75-80 meters from the Maoist camp, which was estimated to have l0-l2 
members, including female cadres. 


As per order, the total strength of SOG were divided into four groups comprising 2, 2, Il, |] commandos 
respectively. Groups 2, 3, and 4 having l2, l] and |l commandos respectively; positioned at strategic points to act as cut-off 
parties under SI (Armed) Rajendra Choudhury. The remaining [2 commandos, including Parida, formed the assault team. 


PART [-.86०. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 743 


Thereafter the Maoists were asked in high pitch of tone to surrender but in vain. When the Maoists refused to surrender and 
began firing indiscriminately and the situation became critical. 


A core assault group led by Hav. Aswini Kumar Bagh, along with Commandos Dambarudhar Bhoi, Nabin Kumar 
Sahu, Pradip Shrestha, and Abhimanyu Majhi crawled into position, despite the risk of severe injury or even death. They 
attempted to return fire from a covered position but faced heavy resistance. Commandos Dambarudhar Bhoi, Nabin Kumar 
Sahu showing exemplary fortitude followed by their team then exposed themselves briefly to inflict fatal injuries on one Maoist, 
despite sustaining splinter injuries and encountering grenade attacks. 


Commando Kamalkant Harichandan, who was not part of the core assault team but was injured during the engagement 
along with other injured personnel, was treated after the operation. In the counterattack, Commandos Dambarudhar Bhoi, Nabin 
Kumar Sahu, Pradip Shrestha, and Abhimanyu Majhi under Hav. Aswini Kumar Bagh's leadership, managed to kill a Maoist 
cadre quickly, which caused the remaining Maoists to flee. The operation lasted about 30 minutes, resulting in the recovery of 
a Maoist body and weapons. 


The deceased Maoist was identified as Akash @ Jaggu @ Sodi Muda, a 35- year-old S/o Sodi Bhima of village- 
Elmagunda, PS-Chintagufa, Dist.-Sukma, Chhattisgarh who was the ACM rank cadre of Udandi/Indagaon Area Committee 
under Dhamatari-Gariyaband-Nupada Division, with a significant criminal record and a reward of Rs. 4 Lakhs on his head. 
This successful operation represents a significant achievement for Nabarangpur's police force, delivering a substantial blow to 
the Maoist insurgency in the region and potentially improving security in this Maoist-affected district. 


In this operation, S/Shri Aswini Kumar Bagh, Havildar, Dambarudhar Bhoi, Commando, Nabin Kumar Sahu, 
Commando, Pradip Shrestha, Commando and Abhimanyu Majhi, Commando of Odisha Police displayed conspicuous 
gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a higher order.

No. |37—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Telangana Police: — 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
Ie Shri Katravath Raju Naik Police Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Shri Katravath Raju Naik, Police Constable, enlisted as a Police Constable in 2006, has displayed unwavering 
dedication and courage throughout his career. On 05.0.2023 at 03:I5 hrs, a gruesome murder occurred on the ORR service 
road at Manchirevula, within the Narsingi Police Station limits. The assailants attacked and killed 26-year-old Seelam Kishore 
Kumar Reddy using a talwar, inflicting multiple injuries that caused his instant death. His associate, Pathloth Shivaraj, was 
also injured during the attack. 


Earlier that night, at 02:20 hrs, PC Shri PV Jyothiram and driver HG Anjilaiah, while patrolling the ORR service road, 
encountered a group of transgenders near Manchirevula. One transgender, Niharika, revealed that two men had threatened them 
at knifepoint for sexual favors. When they spotted the police vehicle, the suspects fled toward Manchirevula. Although the 
police searched the area, they couldn’t locate the suspects. However, the transgenders identified a locked Pulsar bike as 
belonging to the accused. 


Niharika’s well-wishers, Seelam Kishore Kumar Reddy and Pathloth Shivaraj, arrived to assist. The police advised 
them to file a complaint and sent them back on their scooter. While the police continued their patrol, they received a Dial |00 
call at 03:26 hrs, reporting a quarrel on the Manchirevula service road. Upon reaching the spot, they found Kishore Reddy lying 
in a pool of blood and Shivaraj injured. Shivaraj explained that the suspects, whom they confronted, attacked them with a 
talwar, killing Kishore and injuring him before fleeing on his scooter. 


The SOT Madhapur police, PC Katravath Raju Naik and PC Vijay Kumar, tracked down the accused, Sardar 
Chamkorthi @ Karan Singh, in Jagadgirigutta. During the arrest attempt, the accused launched a brutal attack, stabbing PC 
Katravath Raju Naik in the chest with the talwar. Despite collapsing, PC Katravath Raju Naik displayed remarkable bravery, 
got back on his feet, and overpowered the attacker. When PC Vijay Kumar tried to assist, Karan Singh inflicted a head injury 
on him. Still, PC Katravath Raju Naik managed to disarm the accused, pin him down, and apprehend him. 


744 THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) [PART I—Sec. 


PC Katravath Raju Naik was rushed to Ram Dev Hospital and later shifted to Medicover Hospital, where he underwent 
intensive treatment for 8 days before recovering. His courageous efforts led to the capture of the notorious offender, Karan 
Singh and the recovery of the bloodstained pagidi and talwar used in the crime. 


A case was registered against Karan Singh under Cr.No. 6/2023, U/s 307, 333 IPC, and Sec. 25(IB)(b) of the Arms 
Act, 959, at Jagadgirigutta PS for attempting to murder PC Katravath Raju Naik and his colleague. The bravery exhibited by 
PC Katravath Raju Naik in subduing the dangerous criminal, despite life-threatening injuries, bravely overpowered the 
offender. His valorous act not only ensured justice but also serves as an inspiration to his peers. 


In this operation, Shri Katravath Raju Naik, Police Constable of Telangana Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 05/0/2023.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


On 07.07.202l, SI Aksaya Parveer Kumar Tyagi while going to office he came to know that Ajay @ Kalia resident 
of Rewari Haryana, wanted in dacoity cases of PS Naujheel, Mathura is going to Grater Noida to commit some criminal 
incident. On this tipoff, SI Aksaya Parveer Kumar Tyagi without losing any time, informing the senior officers, called his team 
comprising of Constables Rajan Kumar, Mukesh Singh etc. with Govt Vehicle. After discussion with SHO PS Sector 20 Noida 
on phone, he departed and reached under the Flyover of Sector |4 A, where SHO PS Sector 20 Munish Pratap Singh Chauhan 
was present. After planning the strategy, police force started checking. At about |2.l0 PM, two persons were sighted coming 
on a blue Apache motorcycle from the Chilla Border. The informer confirmed them to be the same criminals. Seeing police 
checking, motorcycle riders turned to the left towards roundabout and drove motorcycle very fast. The police team chased the 
fleeing criminals. Seeing the police coming near, the criminals tried to escape leaving the main road and turned their bike on 
the northern track of the dirty drain. Due to a fallen tree on the track, the road became congested and motorcycle was slowed 
down. Meanwhile team reached neared the criminals. The criminals dropped their motorcycle at some distance from the fallen 
tree and opened firing at police personnel. One criminal was firing from a pit under the track while the other was running away 
firing. SI Aksaya Parveer Kumar Tyagi and Insp. Munish Pratap Singh Chauhan challenged the criminals aloud to surrender. 
In response, criminals became more furious and started firing. The bullets fired by the criminal’s, hit vehicle of SHO PS Sector 
20. The bullets fired by the criminals also hit SHO Munish Pratap Singh Chauhan, Constable Mukesh Singh and Constable 
Rajan Kumar. A bullet whizzed past the left ear of SI Aksaya Parveer Kumar Tyagi and he had a narrow escape and was saved 
tactically. Insp. Munish Pratap Singh Chauhan, SI Aksaya Parveer Kumar Tyagi, Constable Mukesh Singh and Constable Rajan 
Kumar without caring for their lives and taking the duty as supreme, displaying exemplary courage and bravery, fired in self- 
defence in controlled manner as a result of which one criminal fell down injured at about 2.20PM, who was identified as a 
dreaded criminal Ajay @ Kalia. He was sent to hospital for treatment where was declared as dead. 


The reward of Rs. ,00,000/- was announced by ADG of Police, Agra Zone, Rs. 50 thousand each by DIG of Police 
Aligarh Range and Haryana police. 


One country made gun small barrel (Ponia) .35 bore, 8 live and 07. empty cartridges .3!5 bore, one country made 
gun small barrel (Ponia) |2 bore, 9 live and 02 empty cartridges, Apache motorcycle No, UPl6BJ-6957,0l hammer, 02 axels, 
4-pointed iron small rods, photocopy of adhar card in the name of Ajay were recovered. 


In this operation, S/Shri Munish Pratap Singh Chauhan, Inspector, Aksaya Parveer Kumar Tyagi, Sub Inspector, Rajan 
Kumar, Constable and Mukesh Singh, Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion 
to duty of a higher order.

No. 39—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Uttar Pradesh Police:— 


SI. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

ly Deepak Kumar Singh Deputy Superintendent of Police GM 

2. Hemant Bhushan Singh Inspector GM 

3. Vinod Kumar Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Vinod Kumar Upadhyay of district Gorakhpur, a notorious criminal and synonym of terror was running his gang to 
carry out his criminal activities. He does not hesitate to kill those who did not pay him extortion money. About four dozen 
cases of murder, attempt to murder, extortion etc. were registered against him in districts Gorakhpur, Sant Kabir Nagar, Basti 
and Lucknow. He was carrying a bounty of Rs. ,00,000/- announced by ADG of Police, Gorakhpur Zone for his arrest. 


An STF team led by DySP Deepak Kumar Singh was collecting intelligence against this criminal. A tipoff was 
received on 04.0.2024 that Vinod Kumar Updhyay is about to go to district Sultanpur to commit some crime. Acting on the 
input, DySP Deepak with his team comprising of Inspector Hemant Bhushan Singh, HC Vinod Kumar and others chalked out 
plans to intercept him. The information was shared with SHO Kotwali Dehat, Sultanpur. Three teams led by DySP Deepak, 
Inspector Hemant and SHO Shyam Sunder were constituted. The team 'A' was positioned on right side of the high way leading 
to Lucknow while team 'B' on the left and team 'C' was positioned on the downhill side of the road leading to Hanuman Ganj. 
All the teams took their positions and waited. At around 03.20 AM, a Grey Swift Dezire car was seen coming from Lucknow 
side, which was confirmed as the car of Vinod Upadhyay by the informer. DySP Deepak attempted to stop the car but the driver 
sped away at high speed, turning towards the semi-finished by-pass road. STF team chased the criminal's fleeing car. 


After a chase of about 2-3 km the car lost control and collided with the semi-finished cement boulder on the service 
lane. The driver quickly opened the door and tried to run away and took cover of earthen mounds lying nearby and threatened 
the Police. DySP Deepak with team got down from their vehicle and took position on the road. Insp. Hemant’s team also took 
position. The criminal Vinod started indiscriminate firing at STF team from which DySP Deepak, Inspector Hemant and HC 
Vinod Kumar had a narrow escape. Taking advantage of the dense fog and darkness, Vinod Upadhyay fired intermittently from 
the cover of the earthen mound. He was repeatedly warned to surrender but he ignored all the warnings and kept on firing 
targeting the teams led by DySP Deepak and Inspector Hemant. Inspite of the danger posed by bullets whizzing past them, 
DySP Deepak Kumar Singh, Insp. Hemant Bhushan Singh and HC Vinod Kumar, showing exemplary courage and bravery 
and using field craft, tried to outflank him, coming dangerously close. In view of the constant firing and finding no alternative 
to arrest the criminal, DySP Deepak Kumar Singh, Insp. Hemant Bhushan Singh showed exemplary courage and bravery and 
returned fire in the difficult situation. As a result of retaliatory firing the criminal was grievously wounded and stopped firing. 
He was thereafter cautiously approached and was sent to hospital to save his life where he succumbed. 


One pistol 7.62mm with magazine and 03 live & l3 empty cartridges, one 9 mm stengun with magazine and 04 live 
& 02 empty cartridges and unnumbered Swift Dezire car were recovered. 


In this operation, S/Shri Deepak Kumar Singh, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Hemant Bhushan Singh, Inspector 
and Vinod Kumar, Head Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of 
a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 05/0/2024.

No. |40—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Uttar Pradesh Police:— 


Sl. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
l. Shri Pramod Kumar Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


In pursuit of operation launched against criminal tribe and nomadic gangs, the STF team was collecting intelligence. 
On 02.07.2020, an input was received that Bawaria gang of nomadic caste is going to commit sensational robbery in the night, 
after crossing the Jewar toll tax on Noida-Agra expressway by driving nails and puncturing tyres on the culvert between 45 to 
55 Km. 


STF team headed by SI Brahm Prakash rushed to the spot with team comprising HC Pramod Kumar and others 
equipped with necessary gadgets. The team contacted SHO Tappal Ashish Kumar Singh who was already patrolling on the 
expressway. STF team reached at Km 50 on the service road. The two teams - one from the force of STF and the other from 
the force of PS Tappal were constituted. The team of PS Tappal was sent to patrol on expressway. The STF team put on the 
security gadgets and started patrolling on service road. 


After going about 03 Kilometers ahead at about |!.25 PM, two suspicious persons with two motorcycles were seen 
standing in light of vehicles at the underpass near Km No. 53. On being questioned, they started firing at STF team from the 
bushes in the east with the weapons held in their hands. The bullets fired by the criminals whizzed past next to the ears of Head 
Constable Pramod Kumar, from which he had a narrow escape. HC Pramod Kumar putting his life in danger and showing 
exemplary courage, combatted the criminals bravely. Team attempted to approach the criminals to arrest them. The criminals 
over looked the warnings and continued to fire. Finding no alternative, taking the duty as supreme, showing exemplary courage 
and bravery and without caring for his life, HC Pramod Kumar entered in the firing range of criminals using field craft and 
tactics to arrest the criminals and fired in retaliation and a sound of groaning was heard and a criminal fell down injured. 


Meanwhile the team of SHO Tappal arrived from expressway. Suddenly two criminals hiding there; fired at SHO 
Tappal who was saved due to grace of God. On retaliation, the criminals started running away who were chased but managed 
to flee taking advantage of darkness, bush and grownup crops. After the firing stopped, a widespread search was carried out a 
criminal was found lying injured, who was identified as Bablu @ Ganja a rewardee of Rs. 50,000/- from UP and Rs. 50,000 
from Haryana. He was sent to the hospital to save his life, where he succumbed. One .3l5 bore ponia CMP with 03 empty and 
02 live cartridges, one ponia CMP 2 bore with 04 empty and 23 live cartridges !2 bore, 02 motorcycles, 02 Bicycle Axels, l0 
nails and file relating to CBI case was recovered from the spot. 


In this operation, Shri Pramod Kumar, Head Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023. 
Consequently, this award carries with it the admissible monetary allowance with effect from 02/07/2020.

SI Mukesh Kumar, Station Officer, PS Muradnagar, Police Commissionerate Ghaziabad U.P., accompanying Police 
team in police vehicle on 02.06.2023 was patrolling for law & order duty in Police Station area and present at Railway Road 
Tri-junction, having received information from his reliable source about arrival of dreaded killer criminal Vishal @ Monu, 
along with his companion on Ganga Canal-track from Chitoda to Muradnagar to commit some heinous crime, police team 
quickly reached at Canal Bridge. 


SOG Team of DCP-Rural headed by S.I.Incharge was found, already deployed, there and meanwhile S.I.Incharge OP 
Railway Road also joined. SI Mukesh Kumar Station Officer within moment, discussed strategy to arrest criminals & deployed

a stopper party near Ordnance Factory Gate and himself, heading Police Team, quickly moved under Railway Bridge on Canal

and fled towards Meerut-Delhi Bypass-NH 58. Immediately, the police team sprang into action and quickly left for Meerut- 
Delhi Bypass to besiege the criminals.

No. |43—Pres/2025—The President of India is pleased to award Medal for Gallantry (GM) to the under mentioned 
personnel of Uttar Pradesh Police:— 


SL. No. Name of the Awardee Rank at the time of Action Medal awarded 
S/Shri 

l. Dharmesh Kumar Shahi Deputy Superintendent of Police GM 

2, Yashwant Singh Head Constable GM 

3. Neeraj Kumar Pandey Head Constable GM 


Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


Dreaded criminal Harish Paswan of District Ballia with a criminal history of about three dozen heinous criminal cases, 
had been a synonym of terror for more than one and a half decades in districts Ballia, Gorakhpur, Mau of Uttar Pradesh and 
bordering districts of Bihar and Chhattisgarh. On 07.07.202I, he brutally murdered Jaleshwar Singh in broad daylight under 
PS Bairiya district Ballia. Thereafter, he was threatening the witnesses of this case. A reward of Rs. ,00,000- was announced 
by the Additional Director General of Police, Varanasi Zone, Varanasi for his arrest. 


An STF team led by DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, collecting intelligence against Harish Paswan, visited districts 
Ballia, Mau, Ghazipur, Noida, Ghaziabad and parts of Bihar. It was learnt that Harish Paswan is planning to kill witnesses of 
Jaleshwar Singh murder case. On 03.09.202!, DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi while developing the input with his team at Mau 
Roadways Bus stand, came to know at about 0.30 AM that Harish Paswan is going from Rasra to Ghazipur via Kasimabad to 
execute the murder. On this, DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi rushed to Chandra Shekhar Azad Crossing Rasra along with his 
team and constituted three teams heading the first. The second and third teams were headed by SI Satyendra Vikram Singh and 
Inspector Satya Prakash Singh respectively. 


The first (striking team) team took position near Neebu Kabirpur on main road Ghazipur via Kasimabad, while second 
team took position !00 meters before the first team towards Rasra and the third one took position !00 meters away from the 
first team towards Kasimabad and waited. At about |.30 AM, two persons were seen coming on a blue Apache motorcycle 
from Rasra side. The pillion rider was confirmed as Harish Paswan. The first team, informing the others, tried to intercept them. 
Seeing the police, the criminals abruptly took a U-turn to run back towards Rasra but seeing the second team cutting them off, 
they turned on the Banauli road. Inspite of repeated warnings to surrender, the criminal sitting on rear seat hurled abuses, took 
out weapons tucked in his waist in his both hands and started firing at police from which DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi was 
narrowly escaped. 


Meanwhile, second and third teams also reached Neebu Kabirpur. DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, HC Yashwant Singh 
and HC Neeraj Kumar Pandey and others alighted from the vehicle and rushed to arrest the criminals who stopped their 
motorcycle before turn to Banauli road and started indiscriminate firing at police. Police party saved themselves by using field 


PART [-.86०८. | THE GAZETTE OF INDIA, DECEMBER 6, 2025 (AGRAHAYANA I5, 947) 749 


craft. DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi again challenged the criminals to surrender and chased them as they fled towards village 
Banauli. The criminal sitting on the rear seat, continued to fire at police with weapons in both hands. DySP Dharmesh Kumar 
Shahi, HC Yashwant Singh and HC Neeraj Kumar Pandey had a close save. The criminals motorcycle went out of control after 
covering a short distance on Banauli road and they fell down. The criminal sitting on rear seat, continued firing while 
motorcycle driver managed to get away with motorcycle in the cover of fire from his mate. All the warning given by the police, 
went into vein and the criminal became more desperate and furious. Seeing the lives of police personnel in danger and finding 
no alternative to overpower the firing criminal, DySP Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, HC Yashwant Singh and HC Neeraj Kumar 
Pandey showing indomitable courage, bravery and passion to duty and putting their lives in danger; came out of the cover and 
moved forward in the cover of slope of the road and fired in a controlled manner. When the firing from the other side stopped, 
the STF team approached the criminal and found him lying unconscious. He was identified as Harish Paswan a dreaded criminal 
and rewardee of Rs. One lakh. He was sent to hospital to save his life but succumbed. 


One factory made revolver .32 bore, One Country Made Pistol .32 bore, 04 empty 02 live cartridges .32 bore 
(revolver), 04 empty and one live cartridge .32 bore pistol were recovered from the scene. 


In this operation, S/Shri Dharmesh Kumar Shahi, Deputy Superintendent of Police, Yashwant Singh, Head Constable 
and Neeraj Kumar Pandey, Head Constable of Uttar Pradesh Police displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to 
duty of a higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


After receiving reliable inputs about infiltration of Pakistani Rangers with a target to make some attacks or to kidnap 
BSF personnel during Operation Sindoor, on border of western front especially in Rajasthan and Gujarat Frontiers, BSF teams 
were activated throughout the western theatre. A team under Ravindra Rathore was also deployed at SHQ Sriganganagar to 
counter any attempt of Pakistani Rangers. 


Ravindra Rathore and his team launched operation ‘Eagle-Claw’ near zero line. His team camouflaged as farmers and 
Maid ambush near the zero line continuously for more than 72 hours. On May 3, 2025, the team detected a Pakistani Ranger’s 
team secretly moving towards zero line by taking cover of wild growth, probably to target BSF party near the zero line. The 
BSF team, under Ravindra Rathore, pounced upon a Pakistani Ranger and captured him, when his team entered in the Indian 
territory. The BSF team had to enter into hand-to- hand scuffle with the members of Spl Ops team as they cocked their rifles 
and tried to fire. 


During the operation, Ravindra Rathore and his team exhibited exceptional courage, operational brilliance and 
devotion towards their duty, which compelled Pakistan to negotiate on the terms of India, with regards to release of Puranam 
Kumar Saw, a BSF personnel, who was captured by Pakistani Rangers on April 24, 2025 from Ferozpur and whose life was in 
peril. 


In this operation, S/Shri Ravindra Rathore, Deputy Commandant, Devi Lal, Inspector, Sahib Singh, Head Constable 
and Kanwaraj Singh, Constable of Border Security Force displayed conspicuous gallantry, courage and devotion to duty of a 
higher order.

Statement of service for which the decoration has been awarded 


In response to the barbaric terrorist attack in Pahalgam on 22 April 2025, India launched 'Operation SINDOOR’ on 
the night of 7-8 May 2025, which involved precision strikes targeting nine terrorists logistics/training sites in Pakistan and 
Pakistan occupied Jammu and Kashmir. In retaliation, Pakistan resorted to heavy firing, shelling on 53 Bn BSP deployed at l6 
Forward Defended Localities (FDLs) across the Line Of Control. During OP SINDOOR, from 7th to l0th May 2025, the 
Pakistan Army launched intense and unprovoked ceasefire violations along the Line of Control targeting Forward Defended 
Locality (FDL) Mukhiyari and surrounding posts with sustained artillery & mortar shelling ((20 mm/82 mm) and medium 
machine gun fire. Shri Alok Negi, Assistant Commandant, Coy Commander ‘G’ Coy, Kandarpa Chowdhury and Waghmare 
Bhawan Deoram of 53 Battalion Border Security Force were deployed at Forward Defended Locality (FDL), Mukhyari. 


As the situation escalated, Assistant Commandant Alok Negi displayed exemplary battlefield leadership by moving 
between forward positions under intense enemy fire, directing defensive actions, ensuring the safe repositioning of personnel 
and personally leading the relocation of mortar under enemy bombardment. Under his command, retaliatory fire, was 
effectively coordinated and delivered on strategic enemy positions including Kopra Ridge, NG Post, DC Post, Sniper Post, and 
JT-l blunting the enemy’s offensive momentum and maintaining control of the area. Executing his orders with unflinching 
courage, Constable Kandarpa Chowdhury, Commander of Mortar Detachment-| engaged key enemy locations such as Kopra 
Ridge, NC Post, DC Post, Sniper Post and JT-l with exceptional accuracy. When enemy retaliation intensified, he swiftly 
repositioned the mortar setup on direct orders from Assistant Commandant Alok Negi and continued sustained fire. His tireless 
leadership of the mortar team for over 48 continuous hours without any rest and relief, ensured uninterrupted and effective fire 
support. Simultaneously, Constable Waghmare Bhawan Deoram, Commander of Mortar Detachment-2; played a critical role 
in executing coordinated mortar shelling. Following instructions from the Coy Commander, he deployed his team under fire, 
delivering high-volume. Retaliatory fire with precision. His unwavering focus and technical skill significantly degrative the 
enemy’s shelling. He also remained in action for over 48 hours, maintaining operatorial momentum and morale. 


. The exemplary leadership of Assistant Commandant Alok Negi & synchronized efforts of Mortar led by Constable 
Kandarpa Chowdhury and Constable Waghmare Bhawan Deoram were instrumental in repelling a determined enemy assault. 
Their seamless coordination, fearless execution under fire; and devotion to duty ensured zero casualties among own troops and 
inflicted substantial damage on enemy fortifications. 


In this operation, Shri Alok Negi, Assistant Commandant of Border Security Force displayed conspicuous gallantry, 
courage and devotion to duty of a higher order. 


The Medal for Gallantry (GM) is conferred under President’s Secretariat Notification No. 85-Pres/2023 dated 
l4th October, 2023 and Ministry of Home Affairs’ communication No. !024/06/2023-PMA dated l6th October, 2023.
